}

#[test]
fn the_language_card_also_compiles_as_a_module_with_qualified_schedules() {
    let source = in_context(&quarry(LANGUAGE).into_iter().find(|block| block.contains("action summarizeRoom(")).unwrap()).replace("schema.kindLabel", "timeline.kindLabel").replace(": nightly ", ": timeline.nightly ").replace(": scheduledSummary\n", ": timeline.scheduledSummary\n");
    assert!(source.contains(": timeline.nightly "));
    assert!(source.contains(": timeline.scheduledSummary\n"));
    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("timeline.q".into(), source)]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
    assert!(guide_context::without_grouped_shape_note(&bound, "groupedMessages").is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.notes);
    assert_eq!(bound.schema.schedules[0].target, "timeline.nightly");
    assert_eq!(bound.schema.schedules[1].target, "timeline.nightly");
    assert_eq!(bound.schema.schedules[1].months, 1);
    assert_eq!(bound.schema.schedules[2].target, "timeline.scheduledSummary");
}

#[test]
fn natural_words_find_their_cards() {
    for (word, target) in [("predict", "prediction"), ("offline", "prediction"), ("pagination", "reads"), ("migration", "deploy"), ("sql", "from-sql"), ("convex", "from-sql"), ("rosetta", "from-sql"), ("push", "effects"), ("auth", "personas"), ("upload", "assets"), ("live", "reads"), ("ordinal", "writes")] {

    }
}

// Wave 6 (LLP 3000.007 §20.1): `guide reads --section pagination` exited 1 and
// `guide recipes --find capacity` found nothing although both are aliases of
// those cards (074, 086). Every alias now works inside its own card, and the
// curated ones land on the section they name.
#[test]
fn every_alias_is_a_section_or_find_word_inside_its_card() {
    for &(word, target) in guide::ALIASES {
        let text = card(target).unwrap();
        assert!(guide::section(target, text, word).is_ok(), "guide {target} --section {word:?} refuses");
        assert!(guide::find(target, text, word).is_ok(), "guide {target} --find {word:?} refuses");
    }
    for &(word, heading) in guide::ALIAS_SECTIONS {
        let (target, _) = guide::alias_section(word).unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{word} is not an alias"));
        let text = card(target).unwrap();
        let landing = guide::alias_landing(word, target, text).unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{word}: no heading of guide {target} starts with {heading:?}"));
        let title = text.lines().next().unwrap();
        assert!(landing.starts_with(&format!("{title}\n({word} -> guide {target}, section ")), "{landing}");
        assert!(landing.lines().nth(3).is_some_and(|line| line.starts_with("##") && line.contains(heading.split(' ').next().unwrap())), "{word}: {landing}");
        assert_eq!(guide::section(target, text, word).unwrap(), landing, "{word}: --section lands where the bare alias does");
        assert!(landing.lines().count() < text.lines().count(), "{word} prints one section");
    }
    let reads = card("reads").unwrap();
    let pages = guide::alias_landing("pagination", "reads", reads).unwrap();
    assert!(pages.contains("### Pages and cursors") && pages.contains("`c: cursor ?`") && pages.contains("`next`") && pages.contains("usePage"), "{pages}");
    let capacity = guide::find("recipes", card("recipes").unwrap(), "capacity").unwrap();
    assert!(capacity.contains("## Booking"), "{capacity}");
    assert_eq!(card("aggregates"), card("totals"), "100 looked for totals under `aggregates`");
    // A whole-card alias that no line names prints the opening, never a miss.
    let oracle = guide::section("read-only", card("read-only").unwrap(), "oracle").unwrap();
    assert!(oracle.starts_with("oracle is an alias of the whole guide read-only card") && oracle.contains("# Read-only"), "{oracle}");
    // Words that are neither an alias nor in the card still refuse.
    assert!(guide::section("reads", reads, "banana").is_err() && guide::find("reads", reads, "banana").is_err());
}

// Wave 6: the bare listing ended in one ~5 KB line of aliases (098); it is one
// screen now, and `guide aliases` lists them one per line.
#[test]
fn the_listing_fits_a_screen_and_aliases_have_their_own_command() {
    let listing = guide::listing();
    assert!(listing.lines().count() <= 10 && listing.lines().all(|line| line.len() < 600), "{listing}");
    assert!(listing.contains("snapback4 guide aliases") && !listing.contains("offline -> prediction"), "{listing}");
    let aliases = guide::aliases();
    assert_eq!(aliases.lines().count(), guide::ALIASES.len() + 1);
    for &(word, target) in guide::ALIASES { assert!(aliases.lines().any(|line| line.starts_with(&format!("{word} -> {target}"))), "{word}"); }
    assert!(aliases.contains("pagination -> reads --section \"Pages and cursors\""), "{aliases}");
}

// Wave 6: 11 lanes re-ran `--section`/`--full` after an index-only opening. A
// piped `guide <card>` (an agent's tool) also prints the first section; the
// terminal form keeps the short index.
#[test]
fn terminal_and_compatibility_overviews_carry_the_first_nonempty_section() {
    for name in KEYWORDS {
        let text = card(name).unwrap();
        let (Some(short), Some(piped)) = (guide::overview(name, text), guide::overview_and_first_section(name, text)) else { continue };
        assert_eq!(short, piped, "{name}: terminal overview also carries the first section");
        assert!(piped.lines().count() <= 150, "{name}: {} lines piped", piped.lines().count());
        let sections = guide::sections(text);
        let (number, first) = sections.iter().enumerate().find(|(_, section)| text.lines().skip(section.2 + 1).take(section.3 - section.2 - 1).any(|line| !line.trim().is_empty())).unwrap();
        let number = number + 1;
        let heading = text.lines().nth(first.2).unwrap();
        assert!(piped.contains(&format!("\n\n{heading}\n")), "{name}: section {number} ({heading}) is printed");
        let (opening, index) = piped.split_once("\n\nSections of guide ").unwrap();
        assert!(opening.contains(heading) && index.ends_with(&format!("--full prints all {} lines", text.lines().count())), "{name}");
        assert!(short.starts_with(text.lines().next().unwrap()) && piped.starts_with(text.lines().next().unwrap()));
        if first.3 - first.2 > 80 { assert!(piped.contains(&format!("… section {number} ({}) continues for ", first.1)) && piped.contains(&format!("snapback4 guide {name} --section {number}")), "{name}: a long first section says how to read the rest"); }
    }
}

#[test]
fn prediction_card_compiles_clean() {
    let text = card("prediction").expect("prediction has a card");
    let blocks = quarry(text);
    assert!(!blocks.is_empty());
    for (i, block) in blocks.iter().enumerate() { clean(&format!("prediction block {i}"), &in_context(block)); }
    let bound = clean("prediction sites", blocks.iter().find(|block| block.contains("mutation saveDraft(")).unwrap());
    for name in ["saveDraft", "post", "guardedPost", "notifyPost"] {
        assert_eq!(bound.schema.operations[name].predicts.as_deref(), Some("eligible"), "{name}");
    }
    for name in ["countedPost", "falliblePost"] {
        assert_eq!(bound.schema.operations[name].predicts.as_deref(), Some("never"), "{name}");
    }
    for (name, class) in [("saveDraft", snapback4_core::schema::SiteClass::Held), ("guardedPost", snapback4_core::schema::SiteClass::Horizon), ("countedPost", snapback4_core::schema::SiteClass::Server)] {
        assert!(bound.schema.operations[name].sites.iter().any(|site| site.class == class), "{name}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn every_alias_targets_a_keyword_and_nearest_uses_aliases() {
    let mut seen = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    for &(word, target) in guide::ALIASES {
        assert!(seen.insert(word), "duplicate alias {word}");
        assert!(!KEYWORDS.contains(&word), "alias shadows keyword {word}");
        assert!(KEYWORDS.contains(&target), "{word} targets unknown card {target}");
        assert_eq!(card(word), card(target));
        assert_eq!(card(&word.to_ascii_uppercase()), card(target), "case-insensitive alias {word}");
        assert_eq!(card(&target.to_ascii_uppercase()), card(target), "case-insensitive card {target}");
    }
    assert_eq!(guide::nearest("pagintion")[0], "reads");
    assert_eq!(guide::nearest("redict")[0], "prediction");
    assert_eq!(guide::nearest("migratio")[0], "deploy");
    assert_eq!(guide::nearest("σ").len(), 3);
    assert_eq!(guide::nearest("banana").iter().collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>().len(), 3);
}

#[test]
fn arrival_recipe_orders_ties_and_retries_then_pages() {
    let source = quarry(card("writes").unwrap()).into_iter().find(|block| block.contains("table conversations:")).unwrap();
    let bound = clean("arrival recipe", &source);
    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
    sent(&engine.mutate("createConversation", Some("create"), Context::new("dev:alice", args(&[])).with_ids(["room".into(), "member".into()])).unwrap());
    for i in 1..=51 {
        let context = || Context::new("dev:alice", args(&[("conversationId", id("room")), ("body", text("hello"))])).at(1000).with_ids([format!("message-{:03}", 52 - i)]);
        let write = format!("write-{i}");
        let first = engine.mutate("send", Some(&write), context()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(sent(&first).field("ordinal").unwrap(), &Value::Int(i + 1));
        let retry = engine.mutate("send", Some(&write), context()).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(retry.state, WriteState::Sent { .. }));
    }
    let first = engine.query("history", Context::new("dev:alice", args(&[("conversationId", id("room"))]))).unwrap();
    let Value::Array(rows) = first.data else { panic!("history is a page") };
    assert_eq!(rows.len(), 50);
    for (i, row) in rows.iter().enumerate() { assert_eq!(row.field("ordinal").unwrap(), &Value::Int(52 - i as i64)); }
    let next = first.next.expect("51 rows have a second page");
    let second = engine.query("history", Context::new("dev:alice", args(&[("conversationId", id("room")), ("c", text(&next))]))).unwrap();
    let Value::Array(rows) = second.data else { panic!("history is a page") };
    assert_eq!(rows.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(rows[0].field("ordinal").unwrap(), &Value::Int(2));
    let outsider = || Context::new("dev:bob", args(&[("conversationId", id("room")), ("body", text("hello"))])).at(1001).with_ids(["bob-message".into()]);
    assert_eq!(failed(&engine.mutate("send", None, outsider()).unwrap()), "E_RULE");
    sent(&engine.mutate("addMember", None, Context::new("dev:alice", args(&[("conversationId", id("room")), ("principal", id("dev:bob"))])).with_ids(["bob-member".into()])).unwrap());
    assert_eq!(sent(&engine.mutate("send", None, outsider()).unwrap()).field("ordinal").unwrap(), &Value::Int(54));
}

#[test]
fn onboarding_recipe_recovers_a_taken_handle_for_the_same_principal() {
    let source = quarry(include_str!("../../../packages/snapback4/CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md")).into_iter().find(|block| block.contains("query handleAvailable(")).unwrap();
    let bound = clean("onboarding recipe", &source);
    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(engine.query("handleAvailable", Context::new("guest:visitor", args(&[("handle", text("alice"))]))).unwrap().data, Value::Bool(true));
    sent(&engine.mutate("createProfile", Some("alice-profile"), Context::new("user:alice", args(&[("handle", text("alice"))])).with_ids(["a".into()])).unwrap());
    assert_eq!(engine.query("handleAvailable", Context::new("guest:visitor", args(&[("handle", text("alice"))]))).unwrap().data, Value::Bool(false));
    let raced = engine.mutate("createProfile", Some("bob-taken"), Context::new("user:bob", args(&[("handle", text("alice"))])).with_ids(["b".into()])).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(failed(&raced), "E_UNIQUE");
    assert_eq!(engine.query("myProfile", Context::new("user:bob", args(&[]))).unwrap().data, Value::Null);
    sent(&engine.mutate("createProfile", Some("bob-profile"), Context::new("user:bob", args(&[("handle", text("bobby"))])).with_ids(["b2".into()])).unwrap());
    assert_eq!(engine.query("myProfile", Context::new("user:bob", args(&[]))).unwrap().data.field("handle").unwrap(), &text("bobby"));
}

#[test]
fn set_state_recipe_predicts_first_like_and_repeated_set_and_clear() {
    let source = quarry(card("prediction").unwrap()).into_iter().find(|block| block.contains("table likes:")).unwrap();
    let bound = clean("set-state recipe", &source);
    let program = &bound.programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == "setLike").unwrap().program;
    let rows = snapback4_core::rows::MemoryRows::new();
    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone())).unwrap();
    for (i, liked) in [false, true, true, false, false, true].into_iter().enumerate() {
        let context = || Context::new("dev:alice", args(&[("postId", id("post")), ("liked", Value::Bool(liked))])).with_ids([format!("like-{i}")]);
        snapback4_core::engine::local::predict(&rows, &bound.schema, program, context(), Ceilings::default()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(rows.rows("likes").len(), usize::from(liked));
        sent(&engine.mutate("setLike", Some(&format!("set-{i}")), context()).unwrap());
        assert_eq!(engine.store().rows("likes").unwrap().len(), usize::from(liked));
    }
}

#[test]
fn direct_and_merged_cursor_generation_contracts_are_distinct() {
    let source = "table events:\n  owner: principal\n  at: time\n  selected: bool\n  by byOwnerAt: owner, at, id\n  cap 10\n  public 'events'\n  sync\nmaintain daily: count events where .selected by owner, day(.at)\n  read <- allow\n  online only\nquery direct(c: cursor ?):\n  return events[viewer] after c first 1 by byOwnerAt\nquery grouped(c: cursor ?):\n  return daily[viewer] after c first 1 by byGroup\nquery merged(c: cursor ?):\n  return merge events after c first 1 by byOwnerAt:\n    [viewer]\n";
    let bound = clean("cursor contracts", source);
    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
    engine.seed([1, 2].map(|day| ("events".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
        ("id", id(&format!("e{day}"))), ("owner", id("alice")), ("at", Value::Int(day * 86_400_000)), ("selected", Value::Bool(true)),
    ])))).unwrap();
    let cursors: Vec<_> = ["direct", "grouped", "merged"].into_iter().map(|name| {
        let read = engine.query(name, Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::new())).unwrap();
        assert!(read.complete);
        (name, read.next.unwrap())
    }).collect();
    let successor = clean("cursor successor", &source.replace("  selected: bool", "  selected: bool\n  label: text <=10 default 'new'"));
    engine.adopt(successor.schema, successor.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
    for (name, cursor) in cursors {
        let read = engine.query(name, Context::new("alice", args(&[("c", text(&cursor))])));
        if name == "merged" {
            let read = read.unwrap();
            assert!(read.complete);
            assert_eq!(read.data.as_array().unwrap()[0].field("id").unwrap(), &id("e2"));
            assert!(read.next.is_none());
        } else {
            let refusal = read.unwrap_err();
            assert_eq!(refusal.code, "E_INPUT");
            assert_eq!(refusal.message, "cursor: cursor token describes a different prefix or generation");
        }
    }
    for (name, doc) in [("feeds", card("feeds").unwrap()), ("orientation destination", readme_reference("feeds"))] {
        assert!(doc.contains("A merge cursor from generation N"), "{name} must scope cross-generation continuation to merge cursors");
        assert!(!doc.contains("A cursor from generation N"), "{name} must not promise it for direct pages");
    }
    assert!(readme_reference("maintains").contains("A direct page cursor binds its evaluated prefix and active\ngeneration"));
}

    }
}

#[test]
fn w9a_expiry_cards_explain_and_select_the_readers_deadline() {
    for name in ["expire", "reads"] {
        let text = card(name).unwrap();
        let prose = text.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ");
        for sentence in [
            "Held device observations republish at `nextExpiry`, including offline; render those rows directly.",
            "Only server-answered reads need a render-deadline filter to hide a returned row while its next network answer is pending.",
            "`client.now()` estimates the server's time and never moves backward; `null` never expires.",
        ] { assert!(prose.contains(sentence), "{name}: missing {sentence}"); }
        assert!(text.contains("const now = useNow(Number.isFinite(deadline) ? deadline : null);"), "{name}: missing server-clock render timer");
        assert!(text.contains("row.expiresAt > client.now()"), "{name}: deadlines must use server time");
        assert!(text.contains("row.expiresAt === null || row.expiresAt > now"), "{name}: missing render filter for server answers");
        let block = quarry(text).into_iter().find(|block| block.contains("query expiring")).expect("compiled expiry query");
        let bound = clean(name, &in_context(&block));
        let query = bound.programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name.starts_with("expiring")).unwrap();
        assert!(snapback4_lang::emit::typescript(&bound).contains("expiresAt: number | null"));
        assert!(block.contains("{ id, body, expiresAt }"), "{} selects its deadline", query.program.name);
    }
}

#[test]
fn w9a_reads_and_refusals_explain_hidden_pages_and_readable_membership() {
    for (name, source) in [("why E_RULE", explain_code("E_RULE").unwrap()), ("refusals", card("refusals").unwrap().into())] {
        let text = source.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ");
        assert!(text.contains("A read denied by a table's rule answers as an empty page, not a refusal, and a client cannot tell it apart from an empty readable page — by design, so that a hidden row cannot be probed."), "{name}");
        assert!(text.contains("For access UI, use a membership query the viewer may read, such as a conversation lookup readable by its members: a member gets the conversation even when its message page is empty."), "{name}");
        assert!(text.contains("An explicit `require … else CODE` still refuses with the program's code."), "{name}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn w9a_held_search_leads_with_coverage_then_cost_then_refusal() {
    let text = card("search").unwrap().split_once("## Search every chat I'm in").unwrap().1;
    let prose = text.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ");
    let coverage = prose.find("With horizon-free sources, `check --verbose` reports horizon-free source coverage for `searchHeld`: it reads one unscoped words index over the messages this device holds, with no membership merge or per-reader copy.").expect("positive source coverage");
    let cost = prose.find("Cold acquisition costs every live message in every held chat").unwrap();
    let refusal = prose.find("refuses E_QUERY_HELD").unwrap();
    assert!(coverage < cost && cost < refusal);
    assert!(prose.contains("Expiring memberships require runtime audience checks; an unscoped matching read can remain non-uniform and `complete: false` below every meter and word limit."));
    assert!(prose.contains("Runtime meter cuts, the non-uniform scope's 32-word limit and page continuation still apply; `complete` describes each returned page, and `fresh` separately describes sync freshness."));
    assert!(prose.contains("The local clients never fall back to the server for a held query, even at a horizon, meter cut or word cut."));
    let wide = explain_code("N_WIDE_MERGE").unwrap();
    assert!(wide.contains("Inspect structural costs with `snapback4 check --cost`; if a held query answers this read, delete the merge."));
}

#[test]
fn w14a_compact_reads_and_wide_note_qualify_word_expansion_and_continuation() {
    let reads = card("reads").unwrap();
    assert!(!reads.contains("at most 32 words per scope"));
    assert!(explain_code("N_SPARSE_FILTER").unwrap().contains("Unknown widths print"));
    let compact = reads;
    assert!(compact.contains("A resumable read-meter cut"));
    assert!(compact.contains("Heap exhaustion clears the whole page"));
    assert!(compact.contains("uniform matching scan is always lazy"));
    let why = explain_code("N_WIDE_MERGE").unwrap();
    assert!(why.contains("subject to final-answer eligibility"));
    assert!(why.contains("Heap exhaustion clears the whole page even during the drain"));
    let search = card("search").unwrap();
    assert!(search.contains("an interrupted existence predicate retries its membership inclusively"));
    assert!(search.contains("same membership again with nothing emitted"));
}

#[test]
fn composition_card_is_reachable_and_keeps_the_routing_boundary() {
    let sync = card("sync").unwrap();
    let paragraph = |text: &str| text.split("A device query returning\n").nth(1).unwrap().split("answer offline.").next().unwrap().to_owned();
    assert_eq!(paragraph(sync), paragraph(readme_reference("sync")));
    for text in [sync, readme_reference("sync")] {
        for word in ["`check --residency` names", "at most one read", "no reading `require`", "found point read", "Composition is never entered while a predictable write is unobserved; the whole local answer stands until it settles.", "every locally kept component"] {
            assert!(text.contains(word), "missing {word}");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn structural_admission_cards_name_every_host_and_the_web_trust_boundary() {
    let boundary = "and a native device checks again; the web device trusts that admission and does not repeat the check, so a hand-built or corrupted backend can still run there.";
    assert!(card("bounds").unwrap().contains(boundary));
    assert!(card("programs").unwrap().contains("E_STRUCTURAL_FANOUT"));
    for code in ["E_STRUCTURAL_FANOUT", "E_UNBOUND_SCAN"] {
        assert!(explain_code(code).unwrap().contains(boundary), "{code}: missing the web trust boundary");
    }
    for text in [card("prediction").unwrap().to_owned(), explain_code("E_UNBOUND_SCAN").unwrap()] {
        assert!(text.contains("the backend compiler's structural admission") && text.contains("hand-built or corrupted backend"));
    }
}

#[test]
fn digest_recipe_is_atomic_bounded_and_reachable_from_the_orientation() {
    let jobs = card("jobs").unwrap();
    let digest = quarry(jobs).into_iter().find(|b| b.contains("job digest(")).expect("a complete digest recipe");
    let recipes = quarry(readme_reference("jobs")).into_iter().filter(|b| b.contains("job digest(")).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    assert_eq!(recipes, [digest.clone()], "one intact digest, reached from the orientation");
    clean("digest", &digest);
    assert!(!quarry(jobs).iter().any(|b| b.contains("job digestLarge(")), "no live paged accumulator");
    assert!(digest.contains(".at >= periodStart and .at < periodEnd"));
    assert!(digest.contains(".ordinal > readOrdinal"));
    assert!(digest.contains("count(messages where"));
    assert!(!digest.contains("first 101") && !digest.contains("table digestMarkers"));
    assert!(digest.contains("once [p.principal, periodEnd]"));
    assert!(digest.contains("for p in profiles:"));
    assert_eq!(snapback4_lang::diag::guide_for("DIGEST_CHAT_BOUND"), "jobs");
    assert!(explain_code("DIGEST_CHAT_BOUND").unwrap().contains("without an effect or marker"));
    let remind = quarry(jobs).into_iter().find(|b| b.contains("job remind(")).unwrap();
    assert!(remind.contains("immutable sentAt"));
    assert!(remind.contains("for i in invites where .sentAt >= periodStart and .sentAt < periodEnd by .sentAt:"));
    // `why` names the job door too, and neither text promises a backfill:
    // missed boundaries coalesce into one run for the latest period.
    assert!(explain_code("E_STRUCTURAL_FANOUT").unwrap().contains("or use a job sweep, which may read a table with expiry"));
    for text in [jobs, readme_reference("jobs")] {
        assert!(text.contains("never twice for the\nsame boundary") || text.contains("never twice for the same boundary"));
        assert!(!text.contains("exactly once across restarts") && !text.contains("exactly once per\nboundary"));
    }
}

#[test]
fn host_unicode_refusal_explains_the_repair_and_search_contract() {
    let why = explain_code("E_HOST_UNICODE").unwrap();
    assert!(why.contains("family store"));
    assert!(why.contains("standard String.prototype.normalize"));

    assert_eq!(snapback4_lang::diag::guide_for("E_HOST_UNICODE"), "search");
    for text in [card("search").unwrap(), readme_reference("search")] {
        assert!(text.contains("browser's normalization"));
        assert!(text.contains("refuses to open"));
        assert!(text.contains("E_HOST_UNICODE"));
    }
}

#[test]
fn maintained_column_notes_do_not_spread_to_existing_cards_recipes_or_templates() {
    let docs = std::iter::once(("language", LANGUAGE)).chain(std::iter::once(("README",README)))
        .chain(KEYWORDS.iter().filter_map(|name|card(name).map(|text|(*name,text))));
    for (name,text) in docs {
        for source in quarry(text) {
            let bound=compile(&in_context(&source));
            assert!(bound.notes.iter().all(|n|!n.code.starts_with("N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN")),"{name}: {:?}",bound.notes);
        }
    }
    for source in [guide::TEMPLATE,guide::TEMPLATE_ONLINE] {
        let bound=compile(source);
        assert!(bound.notes.iter().all(|n|!n.code.starts_with("N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN")),"{:?}",bound.notes);
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#9 — every piped card is complete.
#[test]
fn a_piped_card_prints_whole() {
    for name in KEYWORDS {
        let text = card(name).unwrap();
        let printed = guide::piped(name, text);
        if text.lines().count() > guide::FULL_LINES {
            assert!(printed.contains(&guide::section_index(name, text)), "{name}");
            assert!(printed.starts_with(text), "{name}: whole body precedes the index");
        } else { assert_eq!(printed, text); }
    }
}

// Wave 7: `--section` words builders guessed (008 009 023 065 070 092).
#[test]
fn guessed_section_words_land_on_one_section_even_in_another_card() {
    let land = |name: &str, words: &str| guide::section(name, card(name).unwrap(), words).unwrap_or_else(|miss| panic!("guide {name} --section {words}: {miss}"));
    let wire = land("wire", "acceptance");
    assert!(wire.starts_with("No heading of guide wire is named \"acceptance\"; it is a section of guide testing") && wire.contains("## An acceptance script over HTTP"), "{wire}");
    for (name, words, heading) in [("language", "statements", "## Details"), ("writes", "statements", "## Details"), ("writes", "loops", "### Loops"), ("language", "loops", "### Loops"),
        ("client", "react", "## React"), ("client", "hooks", "## React"), ("client", "React", "## React")] {
        let text = land(name, words);
        assert!(text.lines().any(|line| line.starts_with(heading)), "{name} --section {words}: {text}");
        assert!(text.lines().count() < card(if name == "language" && words == "statements" { "writes" } else if name == "writes" && words == "loops" { "language" } else { name }).unwrap().lines().count());
    }
    // A plurality of mentions lands on one section and names the others; a tie lists the mentions.
    let count = land("totals", "count");
    assert!(count.starts_with("guide totals: keyword \"count\" opens 0. Opening") && count.contains("; also "), "{count}");
    assert!(card("react") == card("client") && guide::alias_landing("react", "client", card("client").unwrap()).unwrap().contains("matchRead"));
    assert_eq!(card("money"), card("types"));
}

// Execute exact guide sources through packed client/native packages, with no checkout binary fallback.
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync, readFileSync, copyFileSync, rmSync} from 'node:fs';
import {tmpdir} from 'node:os';
import {join} from 'node:path';
import {pathToFileURL, fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
import {spawnSync} from 'node:child_process';
const root = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../', import.meta.url));
const binary = process.env.SNAPBACK4_BIN || join(root, 'target/debug/snapback4');
const png = Buffer.from('89504e470d0a1a0a0000000d494844520000000100000001', 'hex');
const invoke = (command, args, cwd, env = process.env) => {
  const out = spawnSync(command, args, {cwd, env, encoding:'utf8', timeout:120000});
  assert.equal(out.status, 0, `${command} ${args.join(' ')}\n${out.stdout}\n${out.stderr}`);
  return out.stdout;
};

test('packed rule recipes run as written and preserve authority at boundaries', {timeout:300000}, async t => {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'sb4-rules4-packed-'));
  t.after(() => rmSync(dir, {recursive:true, force:true}));
  const env = {...process.env}; delete env.SNAPBACK4_BIN;
  const nodeModules = join(dir, 'node_modules'); mkdirSync(nodeModules);
  const pack = (source, destination) => {
    const result = JSON.parse(invoke('npm', ['pack', '--ignore-scripts', '--json', '--pack-destination', dir], source));
    mkdirSync(destination, {recursive:true});
    invoke('tar', ['-xzf', join(dir, result[0].filename), '-C', destination, '--strip-components=1'], dir);
    return join(dir,result[0].filename);
  };
  const unpack = (tarball, destination) => {
    mkdirSync(destination, {recursive:true});
    invoke('tar', ['-xzf', tarball, '-C', destination, '--strip-components=1'], dir);
    return tarball;
  };
  const clientTarball = process.env.SNAPBACK4_RULES_CLIENT_TARBALL
    ? unpack(process.env.SNAPBACK4_RULES_CLIENT_TARBALL, join(nodeModules,'snapback4'))
    : pack(join(root, 'packages/snapback4'), join(nodeModules,'snapback4'));
  const nativeName = `snapback4-${process.platform}-${process.arch}`;
  const nativeSource = join(dir, 'native'); mkdirSync(nativeSource);
  copyFileSync(join(root, 'packages', nativeName, 'package.json'), join(nativeSource, 'package.json'));
  copyFileSync(binary, join(nativeSource, 'snapback4'));
  const nativeTarball = process.env.SNAPBACK4_RULES_PLATFORM_TARBALL
    ? unpack(process.env.SNAPBACK4_RULES_PLATFORM_TARBALL, join(nodeModules,nativeName))
    : pack(nativeSource, join(nodeModules,nativeName));
  invoke('npm',['install','--ignore-scripts','--omit=optional','--no-audit','--no-fund',clientTarball,nativeTarball],dir,env);
  const cliPath = join(nodeModules, 'snapback4/bin/snapback4.js');
  const guide = name => invoke(process.execPath, [cliPath, 'guide', name, '--full'], dir, env);
  const blocks = name => [...guide(name).matchAll(/```quarry\n([\s\S]*?)\n```/g)].map(m=>m[1]);
  const recipes = blocks('rule-recipes'), rules = blocks('rules');
  const {startDev} = await import(pathToFileURL(join(nodeModules,'snapback4/dist/proof.js')));
  const {createClient}=await import(pathToFileURL(join(nodeModules,'snapback4/dist/client.js')));
  const {createLocalClient} = await import(pathToFileURL(join(nodeModules,'snapback4/dist/local.js')));
  const {webDevice,memoryPersistence} = await import(pathToFileURL(join(nodeModules,'snapback4/dist/web.js')));
  const wasm=readFileSync(join(nodeModules,'snapback4/dist/wasm/snapback4_device_bg.wasm'));
  async function until(predicate,label) {
    const end=performance.now()+15000;
    while(!predicate()) { assert.ok(performance.now()<end,label);await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,25)); }
  }
  async function project(t, marker, sourceBlocks = recipes, {schedules = false} = {}) {
    const source = sourceBlocks.find(s=>s.includes(marker)); assert.ok(source, marker);
    const cwd = join(dir, marker.replace(/\W/g,'_')); mkdirSync(join(cwd,'snapback'),{recursive:true});
    writeFileSync(join(cwd,'snapback/schema.q'), source);
    const checked = JSON.parse(invoke(process.execPath,[cliPath,'check','--json'],cwd,env));
    assert.equal(checked.ok,true);
    const notes=checked.notes.filter(n=>n.code!=='N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER');
    if(source.includes('read through live .folder')) {
      assert.equal(notes.length,1); assert.equal(notes[0].code,'N_NO_HORIZON');
      assert.doesNotMatch(JSON.stringify(notes),/last 500/);
    } else if(source.includes('query canReadLadderGrant(')) {
      assert.equal(notes.length,1);assert.equal(notes[0].code,'N_UNREADABLE_PROBE');
    } else assert.deepEqual(notes,[]);
    const host = await startDev({cwd,env:{SNAPBACK4_BIN:'',PORT_LO:'47000',PORT_HI:'47999'},args:['--no-memory','--port','0','--no-watch',...(schedules ? [] : ['--hold-schedules'])],timeoutMs:30000});
    t.after(()=>host.stop());
    const post = async (path, who, body) => {
      const response = await fetch(host.url+path,{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/json','x-snapback-persona':who},body:JSON.stringify(body),signal:AbortSignal.timeout(15000)});
      return response.json();
    };
    const seed = async rows => {const reply=await post('/seed','alice',rows);assert.equal(reply.seeded,rows.length,JSON.stringify(reply));};
    const mutate = (op,who,args={})=>post('/m/'+op,who,{args});
    const sent = async (op,who,args={})=>{const reply=await mutate(op,who,args);assert.equal(reply.state,'sent',JSON.stringify(reply));return reply.result;};
    const failed = async (op,who,args,code)=>{const reply=await mutate(op,who,args);assert.equal(reply.why?.code,code,JSON.stringify(reply));return reply;};
    const query = (op,who,args={})=>post('/q/'+op,who,{args});
    const read = async (op,who,args={})=>{const reply=await query(op,who,args);assert.ok('data' in reply,JSON.stringify(reply));return reply.data;};
    return {cwd,host,post,seed,mutate,sent,failed,query,read};
  }
  await t.test('the compact opening recipe grants only its owner', async t=>{
    const p=await project(t,'table ownRuleRecords:');
    await p.sent('ownRuleRecord','alice');
    assert.equal((await p.read('ownRuleRecordBy','alice',{owner:'dev:alice'})).owner,'dev:alice');
    assert.equal(await p.read('ownRuleRecordBy','bob',{owner:'dev:alice'}),null);
  });
  await t.test('current department transfer maintains every existing child atomically', async t=>{
    const p = await project(t,'table rosterFolders:');
    await p.seed([
      {table:'departments',row:{id:'old',title:'Old'}},{table:'departments',row:{id:'new',title:'New'}},
      {table:'departmentRoster',row:{id:'a',department:'old',principal:'dev:alice'}},
      {table:'departmentRoster',row:{id:'b',department:'new',principal:'dev:bob'}},
      {table:'rosterFolders',row:{id:'folder',department:'old'}},
    ]);
    const replicas = [];
    for (const persona of ['alice','bob']) {
      const opener=webDevice({wasm,persist:memoryPersistence()});let core;
      const client=await createLocalClient({url:p.host.url,persona,device:{open:async(...args)=>{core=await opener.open(...args);return core;}}});
      t.after(()=>client.close());await client.sync();
      replicas.push({client,query:async file=>(await core.call({op:'query',name:'rosterFile',viewer:`dev:${persona}`,args:{file},now:Date.now()})).ok});
    }
    const a=await p.sent('addRosterFile','alice',{folder:'folder',body:'private'});
    const b=await p.sent('addRosterFile','alice',{folder:'folder',body:'second'});
    for(let i=2;i<20;i++) await p.sent('addRosterFile','alice',{folder:'folder',body:`boundary ${i}`});
    await p.failed('addRosterFile','alice',{folder:'folder',body:'over cap'},'E_CONSTRAINT');
    assert.equal(await p.read('rosterFile','bob',{file:a.id}),null);
    for(const replica of replicas) await replica.client.sync();
    assert.equal((await replicas[0].query(a.id)).data.body,'private');
    assert.equal((await replicas[1].query(a.id)).data,null);
    await p.failed('transferRosterFolder','alice',{folder:'folder',department:'new'},'OPERATOR_ONLY');
    const token=invoke(process.execPath,[cliPath,'token','--service','ops','--quiet'],p.cwd,env).trim();
    const moved=await fetch(p.host.url+'/m/transferRosterFolder',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/json',authorization:`Bearer ${token}`},body:JSON.stringify({args:{folder:'folder',department:'new'}}),signal:AbortSignal.timeout(15000)}).then(r=>r.json());
    assert.equal(moved.state,'sent',JSON.stringify(moved));
    for(const row of [a,b]) {
      assert.equal(await p.read('rosterFile','alice',{file:row.id}),null);
      assert.equal((await p.read('rosterFile','bob',{file:row.id})).department,'new');
    }
    for(const replica of replicas) await replica.client.sync();
    const old=await replicas[0].query(a.id), fresh=await replicas[1].query(a.id);
    assert.equal(old.data,null,JSON.stringify(old));
    assert.equal(fresh.data.body,'private',JSON.stringify(fresh));
    await p.failed('addRosterFile','alice',{folder:'folder',body:'old roster'},'NOT_FOUND');
    await p.failed('addRosterFile','bob',{folder:'folder',body:'new roster at cap'},'E_CONSTRAINT');
  });
  await t.test('live parent transfers the packed guide authority, invalidates held reads and moves devices', async t=>{
    const p = await project(t,'table liveRosterFolders:');
    await p.seed([
      {table:'liveDepartments',row:{id:'old',title:'Old'}},{table:'liveDepartments',row:{id:'new',title:'New'}},
      {table:'liveDepartmentRoster',row:{id:'a',department:'old',principal:'dev:alice'}},
      {table:'liveDepartmentRoster',row:{id:'b',department:'new',principal:'dev:bob'}},
      {table:'liveRosterFolders',row:{id:'folder',department:'old'}},
    ]);
    const replicas = [];
    for (const persona of ['alice','bob']) {
      const opener=webDevice({wasm,persist:memoryPersistence()});let core;
      const client=await createLocalClient({url:p.host.url,persona,device:{open:async(...args)=>{core=await opener.open(...args);return core;}}});
      t.after(()=>client.close());await client.sync();
      replicas.push({client,query:async file=>(await core.call({op:'query',name:'liveRosterFile',viewer:`dev:${persona}`,args:{file},now:Date.now()})).ok});
    }
    const a=await p.sent('addLiveRosterFile','alice',{folder:'folder',body:'private'});
    const b=await p.sent('addLiveRosterFile','alice',{folder:'folder',body:'second'});
    for(let i=2;i<20;i++) await p.sent('addLiveRosterFile','alice',{folder:'folder',body:`boundary ${i}`});
    await p.failed('addLiveRosterFile','alice',{folder:'folder',body:'over cap'},'E_CONSTRAINT');
    assert.equal(await p.read('liveRosterFile','bob',{file:a.id}),null);
    for(const replica of replicas) await replica.client.sync();
    assert.equal((await replicas[0].query(a.id)).data.body,'private');
    assert.equal((await replicas[1].query(a.id)).data,null);
    const observer=createClient({url:p.host.url,persona:'alice',waitSeconds:1});t.after(()=>observer.close());
    const watch=observer.observe({kind:'query',name:'liveRosterFile'},{file:a.id});
    const unsubscribe=watch.subscribe(()=>{});t.after(()=>{unsubscribe();watch.close();});
    await until(()=>watch.getSnapshot()?.data?.body==='private','live held point starts authorized');
    await p.failed('transferLiveRosterFolder','alice',{folder:'folder',department:'new'},'OPERATOR_ONLY');
    const token=invoke(process.execPath,[cliPath,'token','--service','ops','--quiet'],p.cwd,env).trim();
    const moved=await fetch(p.host.url+'/m/transferLiveRosterFolder',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/json',authorization:`Bearer ${token}`},body:JSON.stringify({args:{folder:'folder',department:'new'}}),signal:AbortSignal.timeout(15000)}).then(r=>r.json());
    assert.equal(moved.state,'sent',JSON.stringify(moved));
    for(const row of [a,b]) {
      assert.equal(await p.read('liveRosterFile','alice',{file:row.id}),null);
      assert.equal((await p.read('liveRosterFile','bob',{file:row.id})).body,row.id===a.id?'private':'second');
      assert.equal('department' in await p.read('liveRosterFile','bob',{file:row.id}),false);
    }
    await until(()=>watch.getSnapshot()?.data===null,'live parent invalidates held child point');
    for(const replica of replicas) await replica.client.sync();
    const old=await replicas[0].query(a.id), fresh=await replicas[1].query(a.id);
    assert.equal(old.data,null,JSON.stringify(old));
    assert.equal(fresh.data.body,'private',JSON.stringify(fresh));
    await p.failed('addLiveRosterFile','alice',{folder:'folder',body:'old roster'},'NOT_FOUND');
    await p.failed('addLiveRosterFile','bob',{folder:'folder',body:'new roster at cap'},'E_CONSTRAINT');
  });
  await t.test('live asset placement follows the current parent without moving its home',async t=>{
    const source=recipes.find(s=>s.includes('table liveRosterFolders:'))
      .replace('  body: text 1..200','  image: image <=5000000\n  body: text 1..200')
      .replace('cap 20 by folder','cap 1 by folder')
      .replace('body: text 1..200):','body: text 1..200, image: image <=5000000):')
      .replace('{ folder: f.id, body }','{ folder: f.id, body, image }');
    const p=await project(t,'image: image <=5000000',[source]);
    await p.seed([
      {table:'liveDepartments',row:{id:'old',title:'Old'}},{table:'liveDepartments',row:{id:'new',title:'New'}},
      {table:'liveDepartmentRoster',row:{id:'a',department:'old',principal:'dev:alice'}},
      {table:'liveDepartmentRoster',row:{id:'b',department:'new',principal:'dev:bob'}},
      {table:'liveDepartmentRoster',row:{id:'c',department:'old',principal:'dev:carol'}},
      {table:'liveDepartmentRoster',row:{id:'d',department:'new',principal:'dev:carol'}},
      {table:'liveRosterFolders',row:{id:'folder',department:'old'}},
    ]);
    const upload=await fetch(p.host.url+'/assets',{method:'POST',headers:{'x-snapback-persona':'alice','content-type':'image/png'},body:png,signal:AbortSignal.timeout(15000)}).then(r=>r.json());
    assert.ok(upload.asset,JSON.stringify(upload));
    const row=await p.sent('addLiveRosterFile','alice',{folder:'folder',body:'placed',image:upload.asset});
    const get=who=>fetch(p.host.url+'/assets/'+upload.asset.id,{headers:{'x-snapback-persona':who},signal:AbortSignal.timeout(15000)});
    assert.equal((await get('alice')).status,200);assert.equal((await get('bob')).status,404);assert.equal((await get('carol')).status,200);
    const token=invoke(process.execPath,[cliPath,'token','--service','ops','--quiet'],p.cwd,env).trim();
    const moved=await fetch(p.host.url+'/m/transferLiveRosterFolder',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/json',authorization:`Bearer ${token}`},body:JSON.stringify({args:{folder:'folder',department:'new'}}),signal:AbortSignal.timeout(15000)}).then(r=>r.json());
    assert.equal(moved.state,'sent',JSON.stringify(moved));
    assert.equal((await get('alice')).status,404);assert.equal((await get('bob')).status,200);assert.equal((await get('carol')).status,200);
    assert.equal((await p.read('liveRosterFile','bob',{file:row.id})).image.id,upload.asset.id);
  });

  await t.test('membership pages stop at grant expiry without relying on cleanup', async t=>{
    const p = await project(t,'table timedMembers:');
    const now=Date.now();
    await p.seed([{table:'memberRooms',row:{id:'room',label:'Room'}},{table:'timedMembers',row:{id:'member',room:'room',principal:'dev:alice',expiresAt:now+60000}}]);
    await p.sent('memberPage','alice',{room:'room',body:'Private'});
    const client=createClient({url:p.host.url,persona:'alice',waitSeconds:1});t.after(()=>client.close());
    const watch=client.observe({kind:'query',name:'memberPageList'},{room:'room',c:null});
    const unsubscribe=watch.subscribe(()=>{});t.after(()=>{unsubscribe();watch.close();});
    await until(()=>watch.getSnapshot()?.data?.length===1,'held member page loads');
    const before=await p.query('memberPageList','alice',{room:'room',c:null});
    assert.equal(before.complete,true,JSON.stringify(before));assert.equal(before.data.length,1);
    assert.equal((await p.query('memberPageList','bob',{room:'room',c:null})).denied.code,'MEMBERSHIP_REQUIRED');
    await p.post('/dev/clock/advance','alice',{duration:'2m'});
    await until(()=>watch.getSnapshot()?.denied?.code==='MEMBERSHIP_REQUIRED','held page loses authority at membership expiry');
    assert.equal((await p.query('memberPageList','alice',{room:'room',c:null})).denied.code,'MEMBERSHIP_REQUIRED');
    await p.failed('memberPage','alice',{room:'room',body:'Expired'},'E_RULE');
  });

  await t.test('public mirror requires its own image placement and never publishes private notes', async t=>{
    const p = await project(t,'table privateImages:');
    const upload = async()=>{
      const response=await fetch(p.host.url+'/assets',{method:'POST',headers:{'x-snapback-persona':'alice','content-type':'image/png'},body:png,signal:AbortSignal.timeout(15000)});
      const body=await response.json();assert.equal(response.ok,true,JSON.stringify(body));assert.ok(body.asset?.id,JSON.stringify(body));return body.asset;
    };
    const privateId=await upload();
    const saved=await p.sent('savePrivateImage','alice',{image:privateId,note:'private note'});
    await p.failed('publishImage','alice',{source:saved.id,image:privateId},'E_ASSET_PLACED');
    const publicId=await upload();assert.notEqual(privateId.id,publicId.id);
    await p.failed('publishImage','bob',{source:saved.id,image:publicId},'NOT_FOUND');
    await p.sent('publishImage','alice',{source:saved.id,image:publicId});
    await p.failed('unpublishImage','bob',{source:saved.id},'E_RULE');
    const visible=await p.read('publishedImage','bob',{source:saved.id});
    assert.ok(visible);assert.equal('note'in visible,false);
    assert.equal(await p.read('privateImage','bob',{source:saved.id}),null);
    const get= id=>fetch(p.host.url+'/assets/'+id.id,{headers:{'x-snapback-persona':'bob'},signal:AbortSignal.timeout(15000)});
    assert.equal((await get(privateId)).status,404);
    assert.equal((await get(publicId)).status,200);
    await p.sent('unpublishImage','alice',{source:saved.id});
    assert.equal((await get(publicId)).status,404);
  });
