//! Closed admission and schema-fixed prices for transactional live authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.014.000#2-surface-and-admission — never widen an unsupported whole rule.
use super::references::OnDelete;
use super::*;
use reservations::Price;
use through::LIVE_CHILDREN;

pub fn has_live(schema: &Schema) -> bool {
    schema
        .tables
        .values()
        .any(|t| t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.live.is_some()))
}
pub fn parent(schema: &Schema, name: &str) -> bool {
    schema.children(name).any(|(_, _, t)| t.live.is_some())
}
fn nonnull(source: &Table, term: &RuleTerm) -> bool {
    match term {
        RuleTerm::Viewer => true,
        RuleTerm::Literal(value) => *value != Value::Null,
        RuleTerm::Field(field) => source.columns.get(field).is_some_and(|t| !t.is_optional()),
        _ => false,
    }
}
fn scalar(kind: &ColumnType) -> bool {
    !matches!(
        kind.required(),
        ColumnType::List { .. }
            | ColumnType::ListRange { .. }
            | ColumnType::Json { .. }
            | ColumnType::Image { .. }
            | ColumnType::Video { .. }
            | ColumnType::File { .. }
    )
}
fn term(source: &Table, term: &RuleTerm) -> bool {
    match term {
        RuleTerm::Viewer => true,
        RuleTerm::Literal(value) => matches!(value,Value::Null|Value::Bool(_)|Value::Int(_)|Value::String(_)|Value::Id(_)|Value::Bytes(_)),
        RuleTerm::Field(field) => {
            source.columns.get(field).is_some_and(scalar) && !source.engine_field(field)
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}
fn rule(schema: &Schema, source: &Table, expr: &RuleExpr, targets: &mut Vec<String>) -> bool {
    match expr {
        RuleExpr::Public { .. } | RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Deny => true,
        RuleExpr::Eq(a, b) | RuleExpr::Ne(a, b) => term(source, a) && term(source, b),
        RuleExpr::And(xs) | RuleExpr::Or(xs) => xs.iter().all(|e| rule(schema, source, e, targets)),
        RuleExpr::Not(x) => rule(schema, source, x, targets),
        RuleExpr::Exists {
            table,
            index,
            key,
            equalities,
            ..
        } => {
            targets.push(table.clone());
            let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else {
                return false;
            };
            let Some(index) = target.index(index) else {
                return false;
            };
            index.unique
                && index.is_plain()
                && index.columns().len() == key.len()
                && key.iter().all(|t| term(source, t))
                && index.columns().iter().zip(key).all(|(f, t)| {
                    target
                        .columns
                        .get(f)
                        .is_some_and(|k| !k.is_optional() || nonnull(source, t))
                })
                && equalities.iter().all(|(field, t)| {
                    target.columns.get(field).is_some_and(scalar)
                        && !target.engine_field(field)
                        && term(source, t)
                })
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}
fn endpoint(schema: &Schema, name: &str, allow_child: bool, probe: bool) -> bool {
    let Some(t) = schema.table(name) else {
        return false;
    };
    schema.visible(name) && t.ephemeral.is_none() && t.expire.is_none() && t.not_before.is_none()
        && t.recorded.is_empty() && t.maintained.is_empty() && t.written_by.is_empty() && t.sequences.is_empty()
        && (allow_child || t.through.is_none())
        && (probe || !t.on_delete.values().any(|a| a.writes()))
        && !schema.timelines.iter().any(|tl| tl.owns(name) || tl.maps(name))
        && !schema.maintains.iter().any(|m| m.source == name || m.target == name || m.row_key.as_ref().is_some_and(|k| k.table == name))
        && (probe || !schema.receipts.iter().any(|f| [&f.scope,&f.messages,&f.members,&f.runs].iter().any(|n| n.as_str() == name)))
        && (probe || !schema.synced().any(|(_,_,sync)| matches!(&sync.audience, Audience::Target { table, .. } if table == name)))
}
/// Maximum owned encoded key, including tags/terminators and escaped payload.
pub(crate) fn key_column(kind: &ColumnType) -> Option<u64> {
    use ColumnType::*;
    Some(match kind.required() {
        Id | Principal | Ref { .. } => 2 * 144 + 3,
        Text { max } => u64::from(*max).checked_mul(8)?.checked_add(3)?,
        Bytes { max } => u64::from(*max).checked_mul(2)?.checked_add(3)?,
        Enum { variants } => (variants.iter().map(|s| s.len()).max().unwrap_or(0) as u64)
            .checked_mul(2)?
            .checked_add(3)?,
        Bool => 2,
        Int | IntRange { .. } | Time | Decimal { .. } | Money { .. } => 16,
        _ => return None,
    })
}
pub fn key_shapes(table_name: &str, table: &Table) -> bool {
    table.indexes.iter().all(|(name, index)| {
        index.is_plain()
            && name.len().saturating_add(table_name.len()) <= crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_LABEL_BYTES
            && index
                .columns()
                .iter()
                .try_fold(key_column(&ColumnType::Id).unwrap(), |n, f| {
                    n.checked_add(key_column(table.columns.get(f)?)?)
                })
                .is_some_and(|n| n <= crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES as u64)
    })
}

pub fn validate(schema: &Schema) -> Vec<SchemaIssue> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for (name, child) in &schema.tables {
        let Some(edge) = child.through.as_ref().filter(|t| t.live.is_some()) else {
            continue;
        };
        let mut fail = |message: &str| {
            out.push(SchemaIssue { path: format!("tables.{name}.through"), message: format!("live parent: {message}; use guide rule-recipes current-roster for manual maintenance") })
        };
        let n = edge.live.unwrap();
        let matching = child
            .constraints
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|c| match c {
                Constraint::Cap { by, max } if by == &[edge.reference.clone()] => Some(*max),
                _ => None,
            })
            .min();
        if n == 0 || matching != Some(n) {
            fail("declare cap N by the immutable parent reference and retain its exact bound");
        }
        if !endpoint(schema, name, true, false) || !endpoint(schema, &edge.parent, false, false) {
            fail(
                "endpoints must be ordinary nonexpiring tables without derivations, receipt-family roles, private owners, audience-target roles or writing reference actions",
            );
        }
        if schema.children(name).next().is_some() {
            fail("live and captured descendants of a live child are not admitted");
        }
        if child
            .on_delete
            .get(&edge.reference)
            .is_some_and(|a| *a != OnDelete::Restrict)
        {
            fail("the live reference requires on delete restrict");
        }
        if child
            .sync
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|s| s.horizon.is_some() || s.is_union())
            || child.leave == LeavePolicy::DeliveredHistory
        {
            fail("live sync requires one all-current until-revoked audience, without a horizon");
        }
        if child.index(LIVE_CHILDREN).is_none_or(|i| {
            !i.is_plain() || i.unique || i.columns() != [edge.reference.clone(), "id".into()]
        }) {
            fail("missing plain nonunique $liveChildren [reference,id] index");
        }
        let private_key=|fields:&[String]|fields.iter().any(|f|child.engine_field(f));
        if child.indexes.values().any(|i|i.unique && private_key(&i.columns())) || child.constraints.iter().any(|c|match c {
            Constraint::Immutable{fields}=>private_key(fields),
            Constraint::Cap{by,..}=>private_key(by),Constraint::Text{field,..}=>child.engine_field(field),_=>false,
        }) {fail("ordinary constraints and unique keys must use authored cells, not mutable live inputs");}
        if !key_shapes(name, child) {
            fail(
                "live children need plain indexes whose declared maximum keys fit; narrow indexed scalar bounds or use the manual form",
            );
        }
        let mut pending = vec![edge.parent.clone()];
        let mut seen = BTreeSet::new();
        while let Some(target) = pending.pop() {
            if !seen.insert(target.clone()) {
                continue;
            }
            if !endpoint(schema, &target, false, true) {
                fail(
                    "the transitive read-probe graph must be ordinary and nonexpiring, without live/captured, derived or private inputs",
                );
                break;
            }
            if !rule(
                schema,
                &schema.tables[&target],
                &schema.tables[&target].rules.read,
                &mut pending,
            ) {
                fail(
                    "parent authority must use direct scalar comparisons and total keyed ordinary probes; no live hops or optional keys without structural non-null terms",
                );
                break;
            }
        }
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        if edge_price(schema, name).is_none() || write_price(schema, &edge.parent).is_none() {
            fail("live maintenance price overflows its representation");
        }
    }
    out
}

/// Full schema only. Device projections consume exported prices, never rebuild them.
// @ref LLP 3000.014.000#5-reservation-and-accounting-contract — decode, key buffers and retained event storage coexist.
pub fn edge_price(schema: &Schema, name: &str) -> Option<Price> {
    let child = schema.table(name)?;
    let edge = child.through.as_ref()?;
    let n = u64::from(edge.live?);
    let d = crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(child);
    if d == u64::MAX {
        return None;
    }
    let k = crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES as u64;
    let (mut b, mut w) = (0_u64, 0_u64);
    for index in child.indexes.values() {
        let width = index.columns().len() as u64;
        w = w.checked_add(width)?;
        b = b.checked_add(4 * k + 128 * (width + 1) + 512)?;
    }
    let a = u64::from(child.sync.is_some());
    let m = 1024 + 4 * k + 4 * name.len().max(edge.parent.len()) as u64;
    let e = (2 * a)
        .checked_mul(d)?
        .checked_add((2 * a + 4).checked_mul(m)?)?
        .max(reservations::event_envelope(d, a, m, 0).1);
    let steps = (n + 1).checked_mul(
        64 + 8 * (edge.fields.len() as u64 + child.columns.len() as u64 + w) + 4 * a,
    )?;
    let heap = (n + 1)
        .checked_mul(d + k + 256)?
        .checked_add(4_u64.checked_mul(d)?)?
        .checked_add(2 * b)?
        .checked_add(n.checked_mul(e)?)?;
    Some([
        steps,
        2_u64
            .checked_mul(n)?
            .checked_mul(child.indexes.len() as u64)?,
        n + 1,
        n,
        heap,
        0,
        0,
    ])
}
pub fn retained_event_heap(schema: &Schema, name: &str) -> Option<u64> {
    let child = schema.table(name)?;
    let edge = child.through.as_ref()?;
    let a = u64::from(child.sync.is_some());
    let d = crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(child);
    let m = 1024
        + 4 * crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES as u64
        + 4 * name.len().max(edge.parent.len()) as u64;
    u64::from(edge.live?).checked_mul(
        (2 * a)
            .checked_mul(d)?
            .checked_add((2 * a + 4).checked_mul(m)?)?,
    )
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct LiveReservation {
    pub price: Price,
    pub retained_heap: u64,
    pub children: Vec<String>,
}
pub fn write_price(schema: &Schema, source: &str) -> Option<LiveReservation> {
    let mut result = LiveReservation::default();
    for (name, _, _) in schema.children(source).filter(|(_, _, t)| t.live.is_some()) {
        let price = edge_price(schema, name)?;
        for (to, n) in result.price.iter_mut().zip(price) {
            *to = to.checked_add(n)?;
        }
        result.retained_heap = result
            .retained_heap
            .checked_add(retained_event_heap(schema, name)?)?;
        result.children.push(name.clone());
    }
    Some(result)
}

//! Parent authority and lifetime checks shared by the storage adapters.
// @ref LLP 3000.014#4-the-capture — reserve before arm selection; resolve live, stamp, then authorize.
use super::{rules, runner::Runner};
use crate::{
    error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError},
    rows::{Bounds, Dir},
    schema::{Effect, Table, through::*},
    value::{Supplied, Value},
};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

/// Minted only here: the stamped image re-enters the supplied-input channel
/// under reserve_capture's declared allowance.
pub(crate) struct CaptureInput(());

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum ParentVerdict {
    Readable,
    Unreadable,
    Absent,
    Unknown,
}

impl Runner<'_> {
    pub(super) fn reserve_capture(&mut self, table: &Table) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let Some(through) = &table.through else {
            return Ok(());
        };
        if self.capture_prepaid {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let parent = &self.schema.tables[&through.parent];
        self.charge_price(crate::schema::reservations::capture_price(self.schema, table))?;
        self.capture_prepaid = true;
        self.read_tables.insert(through.parent.clone());
        self.rule_dependencies(&parent.rules.read);
        self.rule_dependencies(&table.rules.insert);
        Ok(())
    }

    pub(super) fn capture_insert(&mut self, name: &str, row: Supplied) -> Result<Supplied, RuntimeError> {
        let result = self.capture_insert_inner(name, row);
        self.trace_authorization(&result, name, Effect::Insert, "read through capture");
        result
    }

    fn capture_insert_inner(
        &mut self,
        name: &str,
        row: Supplied,
    ) -> Result<Supplied, RuntimeError> {
        let table = &self.schema.tables[name];
        let Some(through) = &table.through else {
            return Ok(row);
        };
        let mut row = row.into_value();
        let id = row
            .field(&through.reference)
            .ok()
            .and_then(|v| v.as_str().ok())
            .ok_or_else(|| self.rule_refusal(name, Effect::Insert, "read through parent reference"))?;
        let parent = &self.schema.tables[&through.parent];
        // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — the parent lookup is authorization work under the
        // request's physical allowance; reserve_capture prepaid its logical charges.
        let found = self.authorized_scan(&[], |runner, scope| runner.tx.get(scope, &through.parent, id))?.filter(|r| !self.expired(parent, r));
        if self.device {
            let covered = if let Some(found) = &found {
                self.is_provisional(&through.parent, found)? || self.device_work(|runner| {
                    let viewer = runner.context.viewer.clone();
                    let now = runner.context.now.unwrap_or(0);
                    runner.authorized_scan(&[], |runner, scope| runner.tx.recording_covered(&through.parent, found, &viewer, now, scope, &mut runner.meter))
                })?
            } else { false };
            if !covered { return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(format!("read through .{} needs a live, covered {} parent; queued without a row", through.reference, through.parent))); }
        }
        let found = found.ok_or_else(|| self.rule_refusal(name, Effect::Insert, "read through parent missing or expired"))?;
        stamp(parent, through, &found, &mut row)?;
        table.recompute_deadline(&mut row)?;
        if !self.context.is_system() {
            let viewer = self.context.viewer.clone();
            let readable = self.authorized_scratch(self.rule_scratch(&table.rules.read), &[], |runner, allowance| {
                rules::evaluate(&table.rules.read, &viewer, None, &row, false, &mut super::read::PrefixProbe { runner, allowance })
            })?;
            if !readable {
                // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#5 — only the developer trace names the unread parent.
                self.refusing_phase(&through.parent, &format!("insert into {name} needs unread parent {} through .{}", through.parent, through.reference), "read rule");
                return Err(RuntimeError::Rule { table: name.into(), effect: Effect::Insert });
            }
        }
        Ok(row.capture_input(&CaptureInput(())))
    }

    pub(super) fn physical_children(
        &mut self,
        parent: &str,
        id: &Value,
    ) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        self.physical_children_outside(parent, id, &BTreeSet::new())
    }

    /// Whether a read-through child remains outside a delete's cascade
    /// closure. Children in `deleting` are removed first by deletion_order and
    /// therefore do not physically retain their parent.
    pub(super) fn physical_children_outside(
        &mut self,
        parent: &str,
        id: &Value,
        deleting: &BTreeSet<(String, String)>,
    ) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        let children: Vec<_> = self
            .schema
            .children(parent)
            .map(|(n, _, _)| {
                // Only identities from this child table can appear in its
                // index scan. Identities from other closure tables cannot
                // consume a row in this table's prefix.
                let inside = deleting.iter().filter(|(table, _)| table == n).count();
                (n.clone(), inside.saturating_add(1))
            })
            .collect();
        // Reserve the complete schema-fixed probe set before its first seek.
        // Never short-circuit on an occupied table, including at low ceilings.
        // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — the seeks draw from the request's physical allowance; occupancy never selects the logical charge.
        let heap = children.iter().map(|(name, limit)| super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[name]).saturating_mul(*limit as u64)).fold(0_u64, u64::saturating_add);
        let probes = children.iter().map(|(_, limit)| *limit as u64).fold(0_u64, u64::saturating_add);
        self.authorized_scan(&[(MeterKind::Examined, probes), (MeterKind::Steps, probes), (MeterKind::HeapBytes, heap)], |runner, scope| {
            let mut occupied = false;
            for (name, limit) in &children {
                let rows = runner.tx.scan(scope, name, CHILDREN, &[id.clone()], &Bounds::default(), Dir::Asc, *limit)?;
                occupied |= rows.into_iter().any(|(row, _)| row.field("id").ok().and_then(|id| id.as_str().ok())
                    .is_none_or(|child| !deleting.contains(&(name.clone(), child.to_owned()))));
            }
            Ok(occupied)
        })
    }

    // A deadline suffix skips an arbitrary expired backlog in one seek.
    pub(super) fn refuse_live_children(
        &mut self,
        parent: &str,
        id: &Value,
    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let children: Vec<_> = self
            .schema
            .children(parent)
            .map(|(n, _, _)| n.clone())
            .collect();
        if children.is_empty() { return Ok(()); }
        let now = self
            .context
            .now
            .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Input("delete clock is not pinned".into()))?;
        let heap = children
            .iter()
            .map(|n| super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[n]).saturating_mul(3))
            .fold(0_u64, u64::saturating_add);
        // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — three schema-fixed seeks per child table, each drawn from the request's physical allowance.
        self.authorized_scan(&[(MeterKind::Examined, children.len() as u64 * 3), (MeterKind::Steps, children.len() as u64 * 3), (MeterKind::HeapBytes, heap)], |runner, scope| {
            if runner.device {
                let occupied = runner.device_work(|runner| runner.current_children(parent, id, &children))?;
                return if occupied { Err(runner.parent_has_children(parent)) } else { Ok(()) };
            }
            for name in &children {
                let never = runner.tx.scan(scope, name, CHILDREN, &[id.clone(), Value::Null], &Bounds::default(), Dir::Asc, 1)?;
                let finite = runner.tx.scan(scope, name, CHILDREN, &[id.clone()], &Bounds { gt: Some(Value::Int(now)), ..Default::default() }, Dir::Asc, 1)?;
                if !never.is_empty() || !finite.is_empty() {
                    return Err(runner.parent_has_children(parent));
                }
                // §3.3 forbids physical removal even when §6's live probe is empty.
                // The author's implementation ruling keeps this case fail-closed.
                if !runner.tx.scan(scope, name, CHILDREN, &[id.clone()], &Bounds::default(), Dir::Asc, 1)?.is_empty() {
                    return Err(runner.parent_has_children(parent));
                }
            }
            Ok(())
        })
    }

    pub(super) fn parent_has_children(&self, parent: &str) -> RuntimeError {
        RuntimeError::Structural {
            code: "E_PARENT_HAS_CHILDREN",
            message: format!("{parent} at {} has stored children; delete its live read-through children and sweep expired children, then retry `delete {parent}[id]` (guide rules)", self.statement_site),
        }
    }

table staff:

table proposals:
  title: text 3..120
  client: text 2..80
  deadline: time
  state: enum('active', 'signed') default 'active'
  signedBy: principal ?
  signedAt: time ?
  createdAt: time default now
  search title, client
  read <- exists staff[viewer] and (.state = 'active' or exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'partner'])
  insert <- exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'manager'] and .state = 'active' and .signedBy = null and .signedAt = null
  update <- exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'partner']
  delete <- deny
  immutable title, client, deadline, createdAt
  online only

table sections:
  proposal: proposals
  title: text 2..100
  owner: principal
  due: time

  cap 40 by proposal
  immutable proposal, title, owner, due

  update <- .proposal.state = 'active' and (.owner = viewer or exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'manager'])
  delete <- deny

fn manager():
  require exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'manager'] else NOT_MANAGER 'Only the proposal manager can do this.'
fn editable(section: sections):
  s = sections[section] ! else NOT_FOUND
  p = proposals[s.proposal] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require p.state = 'active' else PACKAGE_LOCKED 'This package has been signed and locked.'
  return s

query me():
  return staff[principal = viewer]
query team():
  return staff first 50 by .name
query board(c: cursor ?):
  require exists staff[viewer] else NOT_STAFF
  return proposals where .state = 'active' first 50 by .deadline after c
query library(q: text <=100, c: cursor ?):
  require exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'partner'] else NOT_PARTNER 'The proposal library is reserved for partners.'
  return proposals where .state = 'signed' matching q last 50 by .signedAt after c
query sectionsOf(proposal: proposals):
  p = proposals[proposal] ! else NOT_FOUND
  if exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'owner']:
    return sections where .proposal = proposal and .owner = viewer first 40 by .due
  return sections where .proposal = proposal first 40 by .due
query checklist(section: sections):
  s = sections[section] ! else NOT_FOUND
  return checks where .proposal = s.proposal and .section = section first 40 by .id
query draftsOf(section: sections, c: cursor ?):
  s = sections[section] ! else NOT_FOUND
  return drafts where .proposal = s.proposal and .section = section last 50 by .at after c
query commentsOf(draft: drafts, c: cursor ?):
  d = drafts[draft] ! else NOT_FOUND
  return comments where .proposal = d.proposal and .section = d.section and .draft = draft first 50 by .at after c

mutation createProposal(title: text 3..120, client: text 2..80, deadline: time):
  manager()
  require deadline > now else BAD_DEADLINE 'Submission deadline must be in the future.'
  return insert proposals {title, client, deadline}
mutation addSection(proposal: proposals, title: text 2..100, owner: principal, due: time):
  manager()
  p = proposals[proposal] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require p.state = 'active' else PACKAGE_LOCKED
  require due <= p.deadline and due > now else BAD_DUE 'Section due date must be in the future and before submission.'
  assignee = staff[principal = owner] ! else NOT_STAFF
  require assignee.role != 'partner' else NOT_STAFF 'Assign a manager or section owner.'
  return insert sections {proposal, title, owner, due}
mutation addCheck(section: sections, label: text 2..160):
  manager()
  s = editable(section)
  require s.status != 'approved' else SECTION_APPROVED
  return insert checks {section, proposal: s.proposal, label}
mutation setCheck(check: checks, done: bool):
  row = checks[check] ! else NOT_FOUND
  s = editable(row.section)
  require s.owner = viewer or exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'manager'] else NOT_OWNER
  require s.status != 'approved' else SECTION_APPROVED 'Approved sections cannot be changed.'
  update checks[check] {done}
mutation uploadDraft(section: sections, document: file <=5000000 ('application/pdf', 'text/plain', 'text/markdown')):
  s = editable(section)
  require s.owner = viewer else NOT_OWNER 'Only the section owner can upload a draft.'
  require s.status != 'approved' else SECTION_APPROVED
  d = insert drafts {section, proposal: s.proposal, author: viewer, document}
  update sections[section] {status: 'review'}
  return d
mutation comment(draft: drafts, body: text 1..2000):
  d = drafts[draft] ! else NOT_FOUND
  s = editable(d.section)
  require exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'manager'] or exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'partner'] else NOT_REVIEWER
  return insert comments {draft, section: d.section, proposal: d.proposal, author: viewer, body}
mutation approveSection(section: sections):
  manager()
  s = editable(section)
  require s.status = 'review' else NOT_IN_REVIEW
  require exists drafts where .section = section and .proposal = s.proposal else DRAFT_REQUIRED
  require count(checks where .proposal = s.proposal and .section = section) > 0 else CHECKLIST_REQUIRED
  require count(checks where .proposal = s.proposal and .section = section and .done = false) = 0 else COMPLIANCE_INCOMPLETE
  update sections[section] {status: 'approved'}
mutation signOff(proposal: proposals):
  require exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'partner'] else NOT_PARTNER
  p = proposals[proposal] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require p.state = 'active' else PACKAGE_LOCKED
  require count(sections where .proposal = proposal) > 0 else SECTIONS_REQUIRED
  require count(sections where .proposal = proposal and .status != 'approved') = 0 else SECTIONS_INCOMPLETE 'Every section must be approved before sign-off.'
  update proposals[proposal] {state: 'signed', signedBy: viewer, signedAt: now}

query searchHymns(q: text <=100, meter: text <=32, musicalKey: text <=12, c: cursor ?):
  return hymns matching q where (meter = '' or .meter = meter) and (musicalKey = '' or .musicalKey = musicalKey) first 30 by .title after c
query serviceList(c: cursor ?):
  if exists staff[viewer]:
    return services last 30 by .date after c
  return services where .published = true last 30 by .date after c
query currentWeek():
  start = day(now) * 1d - ((day(now) + 3) % 7) * 1d
  return services where .published = true and .date >= start and .date < start + 7d first 10 by .date
query serviceEntries(service: services):
  s = services[service] ! else SERVICE_PRIVATE
  return entries where .service = service first 40 by .position { id, position, hymn: hymns[.hymn] }

  require exists staff[viewer, role = 'director'] else DIRECTOR_ONLY
  return { total: count(entries where .hymn = hymn and .service.published = true and .serviceDate <= now), recent: count(entries where .hymn = hymn and .service.published = true and .serviceDate <= now and .serviceDate >= now - 30d) }

  require exists staff[viewer, role = 'director'] else DIRECTOR_ONLY
  return insert hymns { title, tune, meter, musicalKey, scripture, topics }

  require exists staff[viewer, role = 'director'] else DIRECTOR_ONLY
  update hymns[hymn] { title, tune, meter, musicalKey, scripture, topics }

  require exists staff[viewer] else STAFF_ONLY
  return insert services { title, date, theme }

  require exists staff[viewer, role = 'director'] else DIRECTOR_ONLY
  update services[service] { published }
mutation addEntry(service: services, hymn: hymns, position: int 1..40):
  require exists staff[viewer] else STAFF_ONLY
  require exists hymns[hymn] else HYMN_NOT_FOUND
  s = services[service] ! else SERVICE_PRIVATE
  return insert entries { service, hymn, position, serviceDate: s.date }
mutation moveEntry(entry: entries, position: int 1..40):
  require exists staff[viewer] else STAFF_ONLY
  e = entries[entry] ! else NOT_FOUND
  other = entries[service = e.service, position = position]
  if other != null and other.id != e.id:
    update entries[e.id] { position: 0 }
    update entries[other.id] { position: e.position }
  update entries[e.id] { position }
mutation removeEntry(entry: entries):
  require exists staff[viewer] else STAFF_ONLY
  delete entries[entry]

    /// queued it must learn `sent`, not `failed`.
    pub fn mutate(&mut self, name: &str, op: Option<&str>, mut context: Context) -> Result<MutationOutcome, RuntimeError> {
        context.generation = self.generation;
        let requested = context.now.unwrap_or_else(crate::now_ms);
        context.now = Some(self.advance_time_audience(requested)?);
        let wire_args = if op.is_some() { context.wire_args.take().unwrap_or_else(|| {
                serde_json::Value::Object(crate::wire::map_to_json(&context.args))
            }) } else { serde_json::Value::Null };
        if let Some(op) = op
            && let Some(outcome) = self.replay_mutation(&context.viewer, op, name, &wire_args)?
        {
            return Ok(outcome);
        }
        // An existing durable receipt owns its id even after conversion to ephemeral.
        if self.schema.operations.get(name).is_some_and(|f| f.ephemeral) {
            self.require_non_media_ephemeral(&context.viewer,op)?;
            self.schema.check_delivery(name, context.delivery_fingerprint.as_deref())?;
            let viewer=context.viewer.clone();
            return match self.mutate_ephemeral(name, context) {
                Ok(out) => Ok(MutationOutcome { receipt_only: false, state: WriteState::Sent { seq: out.seq, result: out.result }, metered: out.metered, tables: out.ephemeral.tables, events: 0, replayed: false }),
                Err(error) if error.is_refusal() => Ok(MutationOutcome { receipt_only: false, state: WriteState::Failed { why: seat_refusal(&self.schema,&error,&viewer), current: None }, metered: Metered::default(), tables: Vec::new(), events: 0, replayed: false }),
                Err(error) => Err(error),
            };
        }
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        if let Some(op) = op
            && let Some(outcome) = crate::store::replay_bound(tx.conn(), &context.viewer, op, name, &wire_args)?
        {
            // Applied between the check above and this transaction.
            let outcome = MutationOutcome::from_receipt(&outcome)?;
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(outcome);
        }
        if let Some(op)=op{assets::require_placement(&tx,&context.viewer,op,name,&wire_args)?;}
        context.now = Some(time_audience::pin_writer_time(
            &self.schema,
            &tx,
            context.now.unwrap_or(0),
        )?);
        self.schema.check_delivery(name, context.delivery_fingerprint.as_deref())?;
        let program = self.programs.get(name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::UnknownOp(name.to_owned()))?.clone();
        if program.kind == ProgramKind::Query {
            return Err(RuntimeError::UnknownOp(format!("{name} is a query, not a mutation")));
        }
        if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action {
            return Err(RuntimeError::UnknownOp(format!("{name} is an action: call it with client.act")));
        }
        if program.kind == ProgramKind::Job {
            return Err(RuntimeError::UnknownOp(format!("{name} is a job: a schedule fires it, or `snapback4 run {name}` does; no caller can")));
        }
        if let Err(error) = validate::validate_inputs(&self.schema, &program, &mut context.args) {
            return Ok(MutationOutcome { receipt_only: false, state: WriteState::Failed { why: error.refusal(), current: None }, metered: Metered::default(), tables: Vec::new(), events: 0, replayed: false });
        }
        if context.presence.is_none() {
            context.presence = self.presence.clone();
        }
        context.entropy = self.entropy;
        context.minter = self.minter.clone();
        let ceilings = self.ceilings;
        let viewer = context.viewer.clone();
        let now = context.now.unwrap_or(0);
        // An `image`/`video` argument names an upload by id; the program
        // reads the facts the server established (`clip.duration`), so
        // they are put in place before it runs. An id that is not the
        // writer's own upload stays as sent and the write refuses it.
        for (name, kind) in &program.args {
            let inner = match kind {
                crate::ir::ArgType::Optional(inner) => inner.as_ref(),
                other => other,
            };
            if !matches!(inner, crate::ir::ArgType::Image { .. } | crate::ir::ArgType::Video { .. } | crate::ir::ArgType::File { .. }) {
                continue;
            }
            let Some(id) = asset_id(context.args.get(name)).map(str::to_owned) else { continue;
            };
            if let Some(asset) = tx.asset(&id)?
                && (asset.owner == viewer || context.is_system())
            {
                // @ref LLP 3000.038#3 — a file argument never reads an image's facts, nor an image argument a file's.
                if (asset.kind == AssetKind::File) != matches!(inner, crate::ir::ArgType::File { .. }) {
                    let wanted = if matches!(inner, crate::ir::ArgType::File { .. }) { "a file" } else { "an image or video" };
                    let error = RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_ASSET_TYPE", message: format!("{name} takes {wanted} and {id} is {}; upload it as {wanted}", asset.kind.word()) };
                    return Ok(MutationOutcome { receipt_only: false, state: WriteState::Failed { why: error.refusal(), current: None }, metered: Metered::default(), tables: Vec::new(), events: 0, replayed: false });
                }
                context.args.insert(name.clone(), asset.column_value());
            }
        }
        tx.savepoint("program")?;
        let mut runner = Runner::new(&tx, &self.schema, context, Meter::new(ceilings, Cut::Abort), tx.next_seq()?);
        let ran = runner.run(&program).and_then(|value| {
            runner.charge_output(&value)?;
            Ok(value)
        });
        // Commit-end expiry can cascade or clear rows that an `after insert`
        // witness names. Final obligations therefore inspect the actual
        // provisional commit state, after every retirement action.
        let ran = ran.and_then(|value| runner.retire_expired_births().map(|_| value))
            .and_then(|value| runner.check_final_exists().map(|_| value));
        let metered = runner.meter.used;
        let tables: Vec<String> = runner.written_tables.iter().cloned().collect();
        let events: Vec<Event> = runner.events.take();
        let action_tables = std::mem::take(&mut runner.action_tables);
        let outbox = std::mem::take(&mut runner.outbox);
        let rows_written = runner.rows_written;
        let mut read_tables = runner.read_tables.clone();
        self.entropy = runner.context.entropy;
        drop(runner);
        let outcome = match ran {
            Ok(result) => {
                tx.release("program")?;
                // @ref LLP 3000.019#4 — P7 reports keep at-most-once custody.
                // @ref LLP 3000.022#6 — a covered report keeps only a receipt at the committed head.
                let receipt_only = rows_written == 0 && outbox.is_empty()
                    && self.schema.delivery.iter().any(|binding| binding.mutation == name);
                let seq = tx.next_seq()? - u64::from(receipt_only);
                read_tables.extend(tables.iter().cloned());
                if !receipt_only {
                    tx.record_commit(seq, now, name, &viewer, program.kind.word(), &tables, rows_written)?;
                    partition::append(&tx, seq, &events)?;
                    effects::record(&tx, &outbox, seq, now, self.dev_effects, &self.schema)?;
                }
                MutationOutcome { receipt_only, state: WriteState::Sent { seq, result }, metered, tables, events: partition::disclosed_event_count(&self.schema, &events, &action_tables), replayed: false }
            }
            Err(error) if error.is_refusal() => {
                tx.rollback_to("program")?;
                let current = match &error {
                    RuntimeError::Conflict { current } => {
                        let mut probe = Runner::new(&tx, &self.schema, Context::new(viewer.clone(), BTreeMap::new()).at(now), Meter::new(Ceilings::default(), Cut::Cap), 0);
                        let value = probe.get(current).ok();
                        value
                    }
                    _ => None,
                };
                let why=seat_refusal(&self.schema,&error,&viewer);
                MutationOutcome { receipt_only: false, state: WriteState::Failed { why, current }, metered, tables: Vec::new(), events: 0, replayed: false }
            }
            Err(error) => {
                tx.rollback()?;
                return Err(error);
            }
        };
        if let Some(op) = op {
            let seq = match &outcome.state {
                WriteState::Sent { seq, .. } => *seq,
                WriteState::Failed { .. } => tx.next_seq()? - 1,
            };
            tx.record_applied(&viewer, op, seq, &serde_json::to_string(&outcome.receipt(Some(op))).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?)?;
            tx.bind_applied(&viewer, op, name, &wire_args, now)?;
        }
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(outcome)
    }

    /// Write rows as the system principal: rules are bypassed, validation,
    /// constraints, aggregates and partition events are not. One commit.
    pub fn seed(&mut self, rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.seed_at(rows, crate::now_ms())
    }

    /// Development hosts supply their clock so seed commits share operation and stream time.
    pub fn seed_at(&mut self, rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>, now: i64) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        let now = self.advance_time_audience(now)?;
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        let now = time_audience::pin_writer_time(&self.schema, &tx, now)?;
        let count = seed_rows(&self.schema, &tx, rows, now)?;
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(count)
    }

    /// Delete a row as the system principal: constraints, aggregates and
    /// partition events apply. Returns the number of rows removed (0 or 1).
    pub fn seed_delete(&mut self, table: &str, id: &str) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.schema.refuse_ephemeral_import(table)?;
        timeline::check_import(&self.schema, table)?;
        let now = self.advance_time_audience(crate::now_ms())?;
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        let now = time_audience::pin_writer_time(&self.schema, &tx, now)?;
        let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
        let mut runner = Runner::new(&tx, &self.schema, Context::system().at(now), Meter::new(if crate::schema::live::has_live(&self.schema) {Ceilings::job()}else{Ceilings::unbounded()}, Cut::Abort), seq);
        if crate::schema::live::has_live(&self.schema) {runner.reserve_bulk_source(table)?;}
        let removed = match runner.delete(table, id) {

import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { dirname, join, resolve } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

// Execute the same guide-boundary witnesses against the qualification's exact packs.
export function proveRules(client, platform, directory) {
  const root = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");
  const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, ["--test", join(root, "packages/snapback4/test/wave14-rule-recipes.test.mjs")], {
    cwd: root, encoding: "utf8", timeout: 360_000, maxBuffer: 16 * 1024 * 1024,
    env: { ...process.env, SNAPBACK4_RULES_CLIENT_TARBALL: resolve(client), SNAPBACK4_RULES_PLATFORM_TARBALL: resolve(platform) },
  });
  const log = join(directory, "rule-recipes.log");
  writeFileSync(log, (result.stdout ?? "") + (result.stderr ?? ""));
  if (result.status !== 0) throw Error(`packed rule recipes failed: ${result.error?.message ?? result.status}; see ${log}`);
  return { status: "passed", log, client: resolve(client), platform: resolve(platform) };
}

    /// lose facts such as a prefix pinned to `viewer`.
    fn scan_prepared(&mut self, scan: &Scan, prefix: Vec<Value>, bounds: Bounds, take: usize, word: Option<&WordRead>) -> Result<(Vec<Value>, Vec<Vec<u8>>), RuntimeError> {
        self.scan_consumed(scan, prefix, bounds, take, word, None, false, false)
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.013#5.1 — domain scalars consume the ordinary authorized scan one row at a time.
    pub(super) fn stream_aggregate(&mut self, scan: &Scan, aggregate: super::extrema::Aggregate<'_>) -> Result<Value, RuntimeError> {
        let expiry = self.next_expiry.scope();
        // Prefixes may be hoisted, and filter/projection locals evaluated
        // before this frame. Without input provenance, conservatively retain
        // earlier unknown coverage. An enclosing page's non-uniformity alone
        // does not make the scalar's inputs unknown; observe its own scans.
        // Ordinary query/component flags remain monotone throughout the scan.
        let inputs = super::components::Frame {
            beyond: self.beyond_horizon,
            unknown: self.missing_fact.is_some(),
            words: self.word_cut_at.is_some(),
            presence: self.presence_unknown,
            ..Default::default()
        };
        let outer = self.components.scalar.replace(inputs);
        let result = self.stream_aggregate_scoped(scan, aggregate);
        let coverage = self.components.scalar.take().expect("scalar coverage scope");
        self.components.scalar = outer;
        let value = result?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.12 — a visible witness proves existence over a
        // per-row rule: rows the caller may not read can only hide absence.
        // Every other gap (a cut, unknown device coverage, a held horizon,
        // words, presence) still makes the answer unavailable.
        let witnessed = scan.existence && matches!(value, Value::Int(n) if n > 0)
            && super::components::Frame { non_uniform: false, ..coverage.clone() }.coverage().complete;
        if !coverage.coverage().complete && !witnessed {
            self.scalar_unavailable = true;
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(format!("{}: streaming aggregate needs complete domain coverage", scan.site)));
        }
        expiry.emit();
        Ok(value)
    }

    fn stream_aggregate_scoped(&mut self, scan: &Scan, mut aggregate: super::extrema::Aggregate<'_>) -> Result<Value, RuntimeError> {
        self.schema.refuse_private(&scan.table)?;
        let prefix = self.prefix_values(scan)?;
        let table = &self.schema.tables[&scan.table];
        let index = &table.indexes[&scan.index];
        let bounds = self.bounds(scan, table, index, Some(&prefix))?;
        self.page_depth += 1;
        let result = self.scan_consumed(scan, prefix, bounds, self.take(&scan.take)?, None, Some(&mut aggregate), false, false);
        self.page_depth -= 1;
        result?;
        if scan.existence && aggregate.is_zero() && self.device && table.sync.as_ref().is_some_and(|s| s.horizon.is_some()) {
            let prefix = self.prefix_values(scan)?;
            let retained = self.retained_group(&scan.table, &scan.index, &prefix)?;
            self.horizon_coverage(scan, &prefix, retained);
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.013#5.1 — a domain total has no partial scalar answer, including on a device with unknown coverage.
        self.meter.require_uncapped()?;
        aggregate.finish()
    }

    fn authorized_candidate(&mut self, scan: &Scan, prefix: &[Value], bounds: &Bounds, dir: Dir, remaining: &mut usize) -> Result<Vec<(Option<super::authorized_point::AuthorizedRow<'r>>, Vec<u8>)>, RuntimeError> {

    pub fn run_job_chunk_for(&mut self, now: i64, budget: std::time::Duration) -> Result<Option<JobRun>, RuntimeError> {
        let started = std::time::Instant::now();
        let presence = self.presence.clone();
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.036.003#3.2 — a job's
        // runner, partition events and commit share the floor pinned inside
        // their writer transaction, for Quarry and native chunks alike.
        let now = super::time_audience::pin_writer_time(&self.schema, &tx, now)?;
        let head = tx.conn().query_row(&format!("SELECT {COLUMNS} FROM _jobs WHERE state IN ('queued', 'running') ORDER BY created_at, id LIMIT 1"), [], read_head).optional()?;
        let Some(mut head) = head else { return Ok(None) };
        let program = self.programs.get(&head.run.name).cloned();
        #[cfg(feature = "native")]
        let current_hash = program.as_ref().map(|program| job_admission_hash(program, self.native.as_deref().map(super::native::Module::digest)));
        #[cfg(not(feature = "native"))]

        let Some(program) = program.filter(|_| current_hash.as_deref() == Some(&head.program_hash)) else {
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _jobs SET state = 'parked', error = ?2, updated_at = ?3 WHERE id = ?1", params![head.run.id, format!("E_JOB_GENERATION: job {} or its native module changed (or the run predates module binding) since this run was queued; queue it again", head.run.name), now])?;

    /// fired for is committed with the job row, so a restart never fires
    /// twice for one period; a schedule seen for the first time fires from
    /// its next boundary. Returns the names fired.
    pub fn fire_schedules(&mut self, now: i64) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        self.fire_schedule_range(now, 0..self.schema.schedules.len(), None)
    }

    /// One atomic schedule boundary, so the host can release its writer between
    /// schedules. A changed/shortened schema is revisited on the next tick.
    pub fn fire_schedule_at(&mut self, now: i64, index: usize) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        self.fire_schedule_range(now, index..index.saturating_add(1), None)
    }

    /// Admit one historical period on the current clock. In particular an
    /// action's deadline begins at admission, not at the historical boundary.
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12.1 — period identity and admission time are distinct during explicit catch-up.
    pub fn fire_schedule_boundary_at(&mut self, now: i64, index: usize, boundary: i64) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        let schedule = self.schema.schedules.get(index).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Input("unknown schedule index".into()))?;
        if schedule.boundary(boundary) != boundary || boundary > schedule.boundary(now) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Input("schedule window must end at an elapsed boundary".into()));
        }
        self.fire_schedule_range(now, index..index.saturating_add(1), Some(boundary))
    }

    fn fire_schedule_range(&mut self, now: i64, range: std::ops::Range<usize>, selected: Option<i64>) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        let mut fired = Vec::new();
        for schedule in self.schema.schedules.get(range).unwrap_or(&[]).to_vec() {
            let boundary = selected.unwrap_or_else(|| schedule.boundary(now));
            let key = format!("schedule:{}", schedule.name);
            let shape_key = format!("schedule-shape:{}", schedule.name);
            let shape = digest(&schedule);
            let program = self.programs.get(&schedule.target).filter(|p| matches!(p.kind, ProgramKind::Job | ProgramKind::Action))
                .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("schedule {} names missing job or action {}; restore its target before firing it", schedule.name, schedule.target)))?.clone();
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action && !schedule.service.as_deref().is_some_and(crate::identity::is_service) {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("a scheduled action requires its declared service seat".into()));
            }
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Job && schedule.service.is_some() {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("a scheduled job runs as the system, not a service seat".into()));
            }
            let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
            let previous_shape: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1", [&shape_key], |row| row.get(0)).optional()?;
            if previous_shape.as_ref().is_some_and(|previous| previous != &shape) {
                tx.set_meta(&key, &boundary.to_string())?;
                tx.set_meta(&shape_key, &shape)?;
                tx.commit()?;
                continue;
            }
            tx.set_meta(&shape_key, &shape)?;
            let last: Option<i64> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1", [&key], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).optional()?.and_then(|value| value.parse().ok());
            if last.is_some_and(|last| last >= boundary) {
                tx.commit()?;
                continue;
            }
            let Some(_) = last else {
                tx.set_meta(&key, &boundary.to_string())?;
                tx.commit()?;
                continue;
            };
            let mut args = BTreeMap::new();
            if program.args.contains_key("periodStart") {
                args.insert("periodStart".to_owned(), Value::Int(schedule.previous(boundary)));
            }
            if program.args.contains_key("periodEnd") {
                args.insert("periodEnd".to_owned(), Value::Int(boundary));
            }
            let id = crate::ids::mint(now, &mut self.minter)?;
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action {
                let principal = schedule.service.as_deref().expect("checked service seat");
                validate_inputs(&self.schema, &program, &mut args)?;
                super::actions::authorize(&program, principal)?;
                let raw = serde_json::Value::Object(crate::wire::map_to_json(&args));
                let context = Context::new(principal, args).at(now);
                // @ref LLP 3000.009#5.2 — the boundary and service-seat admission are one commit.
                super::actions::insert_request(&tx, &self.schema, &program, &id, &context, &raw, true, now)?;
            } else {
                #[cfg(feature = "native")]
                let program_hash = job_admission_hash(&program, self.native.as_deref().map(super::native::Module::digest));
                #[cfg(not(feature = "native"))]
                let program_hash = job_admission_hash(&program, None);
                insert_job(tx.conn(), &id, &schedule.target, &args, &program_hash, now)?;
            }
            tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _meta(key, value) VALUES (?1, ?2) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value", params![key, boundary.to_string()])?;
            tx.commit()?;
            fired.push(schedule.name.clone());
        }
        Ok(fired)
    }

//! Atomic maintenance of bounded live-parent authority copies.
// @ref LLP 3000.014.000#4-atomic-maintenance-algorithm — one source attempt owns the complete fanout reservation.
use super::{runner::Runner, write::Origin};
use crate::{
    error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError},
    meter::{Ceilings, Cut, Meter},
    rows::{Bounds, Dir},
    schema::{live, through::LIVE_CHILDREN},
    value::Value,
};

impl Runner<'_> {
    pub(super) fn live_read_dependency(&mut self, name: &str) {
        if let Some(edge) = self
            .schema
            .tables
            .get(name)
            .and_then(|t| t.through.as_ref())
            .filter(|t| t.live.is_some())
        {
            self.read_tables.insert(edge.parent.clone());
            self.rule_dependencies(&self.schema.tables[&edge.parent].rules.read);
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn reserve_live(&mut self, source: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        if self.live_prepaid {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let plan = self.schema.write_reservation(source);
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        let plan = if self.device {
            plan
        } else {
            crate::schema::reservations::reservation(self.schema, source)
        };
        let price = plan.and_then(|p| p.live);
        if self.device && (price.is_some() || live::parent(self.schema, source)) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("live parent authority changes need atomic server maintenance; queued without predictions".into()));
        }
        if let Some(price) = price {
            self.charge_price(price.price)?;
            // Schema-selected disclosure mask, independent of live occupancy.
            self.action_tables.extend(price.children.iter().cloned());
            self.live_heap = price.price[4].saturating_sub(price.retained_heap);
            self.live_prepaid = true;
        } else if live::parent(self.schema, source) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(
                "live parent has no complete source reservation".into(),
            ));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    pub(super) fn maintain_live(
        &mut self,
        source: &str,
        image: &Value,
    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        if !live::parent(self.schema, source) {
            return Ok(());
        }
        if self.device {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(
                "live parent maintenance runs on the server".into(),
            ));
        }
        if !self.live_prepaid {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Store(
                "live parent write bypassed its source reservation".into(),
            ));
        }
        let children: Vec<_> = self
            .schema
            .children(source)
            .filter(|(_, _, t)| t.live.is_some())
            .map(|(n, _, _)| n.clone())
            .collect();
        for name in children {
            self.written_tables.insert(name.clone());
            let table = &self.schema.tables[&name];
            let edge = table.through.as_ref().expect("live edge");
            let n = edge.live.expect("live bound") as usize;
            let price = live::edge_price(self.schema, &name)
                .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema("invalid live maintenance price".into()))?;
            if !self.context.is_system() {
                self.tx.draw(&name, price[2])?;
            }
            let ceiling = Ceilings {
                steps: price[0],
                index_entries: price[1],
                examined: price[2],
                rows_written: price[3],
                heap_bytes: price[4],
                output_bytes: price[5],
                rule_probes: price[6],
            };
            let saved = std::mem::replace(&mut self.meter, Meter::new(ceiling, Cut::Abort));
            let prepaid = self.tx.reference_prepaid(true);
            let result = (|| {
                // Price includes retained scan, all simultaneous key vectors and
                // event bookkeeping before any private image is materialized.
                self.meter.heap(price[4])?;
                self.meter.charge(MeterKind::Examined, price[2])?;
                let id = image.field("id")?.clone();
                let found = self.metered_scan(|r, scope| {
                    r.tx.scan(
                        scope,
                        &name,
                        LIVE_CHILDREN,
                        &[id],
                        &Bounds::default(),
                        Dir::Asc,
                        n + 1,
                    )
                })?;
                if found.len() > n {
                    return Err(RuntimeError::Store(
                        "live parent physical cap invariant failed".into(),
                    ));
                }
                for (old, _) in found {
                    self.meter
                        .step(edge.fields.len() as u64 + table.columns.len() as u64 + 8)?;
                    let mut new = old.clone();
                    super::through::stamp(&self.schema.tables[source], edge, image, &mut new)?;
                    table.recompute_deadline(&mut new)?;
                    if old == new {
                        continue;
                    }
                    // All authored cells and constraint keys are unchanged.
                    // Only this origin can replace mapped engine inputs.
                    let entries = self.tx.put(&name, table, &new, Some(&old))?;
                    self.meter.charge(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
                    self.row_changed(&name, Some(&old), Some(&new), Origin::LiveAuthority)?;
                }
                Ok(())
            })();
            self.tx.reference_prepaid(prepaid);
            self.meter = saved;
            if result.is_err() {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Store("live parent authority invariant or maintenance reservation failed; transaction rolled back".into()));
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
    pub(super) fn maintain_live(
        &mut self,
        source: &str,
        _image: &Value,
    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        if live::parent(self.schema, source) {
            Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(
                "live parent maintenance runs on the server".into(),
            ))
        } else {
            Ok(())
        }
    }
}

    pub(super) fn insert_upsert(&mut self, table_name: &str, addressed_index: &str, row: Supplied) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.refuse_upsert_owned(table_name)?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#5 — trusted fixture imports preserve the constraint layer.
        if self.context.is_system() {
            return self.insert_attempt(table_name, row, false, Some(addressed_index)).map(|_| ()).map_err(|error| match error {
                RuntimeError::Input(message) => RuntimeError::Constraint(message),
                other => other,
            });
        }
        if self.schema.tables[table_name].through.is_some() {
            // A read-through child's insert arm keeps the hidden-target word for every authority refusal.
            return self.insert_attempt(table_name, row, false, Some(addressed_index)).map(|_| ()).map_err(|e| match e {
                RuntimeError::Rule {..} | RuntimeError::Input(_) => self.no_write_target(), other => other,
            });
        }
        match self.insert_attempt(table_name, row, false, Some(addressed_index)).map(|_| ()) {
            Err(RuntimeError::Input(_)) => Err(self.no_write_target()),
            result => result,
        }
    }

    fn replace(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: Value, new: Value, readable: bool, patch: &Value, copy: u64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.authorize_update_fields(table_name, &old, &new, patch, readable)?;
        self.replace_authorized(table_name, old, new, readable, patch, copy)
    }

    /// `copy` is the merged image's lawful price, charged again for an engine slot copy.
    pub(super) fn replace_authorized(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: Value, new: Value, readable: bool, patch: &Value, copy: u64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.refuse_engine_owned(table_name, Effect::Update)?;
        self.refuse_receipt_fields(table_name, patch, false)?;
        self.refuse_recorded(table_name, patch)?;
        super::sequences::refuse_fields(&self.schema.tables[table_name], table_name, patch)?;
        self.authorize_fields(table_name, &old, patch)?;
        let definition = &self.schema.tables[table_name];
        for path in definition.columns.keys().filter(|field| {
            definition.engine_field(field) && field.as_str() != crate::schema::through::DEADLINE
        }) {
            if old.field(path).ok() != new.field(path).ok() {
                return Err(if readable { RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_RECORDED_FIELD", message: format!("engine replacement changed stored attribution at {table_name}.{path} (guide maintains)") } } else { self.no_write_target() });
            }
        }
        let mut new = new;
        self.schema.tables[table_name].recompute_deadline(&mut new)?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.017#2.2 — extending life re-admits the new image.
        if self.schema.tables[table_name].stamp_ephemeral(&mut new, self.context.now.unwrap_or(0))?
            && !self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &new)? {
            return Err(if readable { RuntimeError::Rule { table: table_name.into(), effect: Effect::Update } } else { self.no_write_target() });
        }
        let new = self.with_asset_facts(table_name, new, Some(&old), (!readable).then_some(patch))?;
        if let Err(error) = super::validate::validate_projection(self.schema, table_name, &new, self.device.then_some(self.context.viewer.as_str())) { return Err(if readable { error } else { self.no_write_target() }); }

//! Rule evaluation and the static facts about it that the engine hoists: a
//! rule that is proven true by the scanned prefix (`decided_true`) costs no
//! probes; a rule whose deciding fields are pinned by the prefix (`uniform`) is
//! decided once for the whole range. Copied from Snapback 2's uniformity
//! analysis (LLP 2000.000 §5.1); the certificate that priced these is gone,
//! the analysis is not.

use crate::ir::{Bound, Expr, IndexKeyExpr, Input, Merge, Scan};
use crate::schema::{ColumnType, RuleExpr, RuleTerm, Schema, Table};
use crate::value::Value;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Visibility {
    /// One verdict serves every row of the scanned prefix.
    pub uniform: bool,
    /// That verdict is `true` before any row is read.
    pub decided_true: bool,
}

pub fn scan_visibility(schema: &Schema, scan: &Scan) -> Visibility {
    let Some(table) = schema.table(&scan.table) else { return Visibility::default() };
    let Some(index) = table.index(&scan.index) else { return Visibility::default() };
    let pinned = |name: &str| index.components.iter().zip(&scan.prefix).find_map(|(part, key)| (part.column().is_some_and(|column| column == name)).then(|| key.scalar()).flatten());
    visibility(&scan.table, table, &table.rules.read, &pinned)

}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.5 — a missing upsert may insert only when the complete held group and its key decide that no hidden target can exist.
pub fn upsert_absence(schema: &Schema, name: &str, index: &str, key: &[Expr]) -> Result<Visibility, &'static str> {
    let table = schema.table(name).ok_or("the target is online only")?;
    let sync = table.sync.as_ref().ok_or("the target is online only")?;
    if sync.horizon.is_some() { return Err("the target has a horizon"); }
    let rule = table.rules.read.device_audience_rule();
    // Public subset sync has no whole-table absence proof. Its group coverage
    // is a separate decision; do not infer it merely from the public read rule.
    if rule.is_uniformly_readable() && sync.audience != crate::schema::Audience::Public { return Err("the key's synced group is not established"); }
    let index = table.index(index).ok_or("the key does not pin readability")?;
    let pinned = crate::small_map::collect(index.components.iter().zip(key).filter_map(|(part, expression)| part.column().map(|column| (column, expression))));
    let visibility = prefix_visibility(name, table, &rule, &pinned);
    if !visibility.uniform { return Err("the key does not pin readability"); }
    Ok(visibility)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — simplify self-witnesses, then pre-check only the leading conjuncts whose fields the scope pins, in evaluation order.
pub(super) fn prefix_decision(schema: &Schema, scan: &Scan) -> Option<RuleExpr> {
    let table = schema.table(&scan.table)?;
    let index = table.index(&scan.index)?;
    let pinned: BTreeMap<_,_> = crate::small_map::collect(index.components.iter().zip(&scan.prefix).filter_map(|(part, key)| part.column().zip(key.scalar())));
    if table.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| pinned.contains_key(&t.reference)) { return Some(table.rules.read.clone()); }
    let rule = simplify_prefix(&table.rules.read, &scan.table, table, &pinned);
    if matches!(rule, RuleExpr::Allow) { return None; }
    let mut terms = Vec::new();
    conjuncts(rule, &mut terms);
    let decided: Vec<_> = terms.into_iter().take_while(|term| {
        let mut fields = BTreeSet::new();
        term.fields(&mut fields);
        fields.iter().all(|field| pinned.contains_key(field))
    }).collect();
    (!decided.is_empty()).then_some(RuleExpr::And(decided))
}

// Flatten only conjunctions, preserving left-to-right short-circuit order.
fn conjuncts(rule: RuleExpr, out: &mut Vec<RuleExpr>) {
    match rule { RuleExpr::And(terms) => for term in terms { conjuncts(term, out); }, rule => out.push(rule) }
}

fn simplify_prefix(rule: &RuleExpr, name: &str, table: &Table, pinned: &BTreeMap<&String, &Expr>) -> RuleExpr {
    let pin = |name: &str| pinned.iter().find_map(|(key, value)| (key.as_str() == name).then_some(*value));
    if rule_known(rule, true, name, table, &pin) { return RuleExpr::Allow; }
    if rule_known(rule, false, name, table, &pin) { return RuleExpr::Deny; }
    match rule {
        RuleExpr::And(terms) => {
            let mut out = Vec::new();
            for term in terms { conjuncts(simplify_prefix(term, name, table, pinned), &mut out); }
            out.retain(|term| !matches!(term, RuleExpr::Allow));
            RuleExpr::And(out)
        },
        RuleExpr::Or(_) => {
            // Use the same self-witness absorption as uniformity, but remove
            // the absorbed field read from the expression we actually run.
            let terms = rule.disjuncts();
            let mut out: Vec<_> = terms.iter()
                .filter(|term| !terms.iter().any(|other| self_probe_absorbs(name, table, term, other)))
                .map(|term| simplify_prefix(term, name, table, pinned))
                .filter(|term| !matches!(term, RuleExpr::Deny))
                .collect();
            match out.len() {
                0 => RuleExpr::Deny,
                1 => out.pop().unwrap(),
                _ => RuleExpr::Or(out),
            }
        }
        RuleExpr::Not(inner) => {
            let inner = simplify_prefix(inner, name, table, pinned);
            negate_required_bool(&inner, table).unwrap_or_else(|| RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(inner)))
        }
        RuleExpr::Ne(left, right) => required_bool_equality(left, right, table, false).unwrap_or_else(|| rule.clone()),
        _ => rule.clone(),
    }
}

/// Required booleans have exactly two values, so their negated equality and
/// inequality forms can share one positive equality identity. Optional bools
/// retain their original expression because `null` is distinct from `false`.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#9 — prefix simplification and guard coverage use the same required-Boolean identity.
fn required_bool_equality(left: &RuleTerm, right: &RuleTerm, table: &Table, equal: bool) -> Option<RuleExpr> {
    [(left, right), (right, left)].into_iter().find_map(|(field, value)| {
        let (RuleTerm::Field(column), RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Bool(expected))) = (field, value) else { return None };
        matches!(table.columns.get(column), Some(ColumnType::Bool)).then(|| RuleExpr::Eq(
            RuleTerm::Field(column.clone()),
            RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Bool(if equal { *expected } else { !*expected })),
        ))
    })
}

fn negate_required_bool(rule: &RuleExpr, table: &Table) -> Option<RuleExpr> {
    match rule {
        RuleExpr::Allow => Some(RuleExpr::Deny),
        RuleExpr::Deny => Some(RuleExpr::Allow),

# Rule recipes: audiences, disclosure and blind writers

These compiled recipes express roles, copied audiences, consent, publication, link enrollment and anonymity. Each makes the intended disclosure and write authority explicit, including the trade-offs of materialising a grant.

```quarry
use personas alice, bob, carol
table ownRuleRecords:
  owner: principal
  unique owner
  read <- .owner = viewer
  insert <- .owner = viewer
  online only
mutation ownRuleRecord():
  return insert ownRuleRecords { owner: viewer }
query ownRuleRecordBy(owner: principal):
  return ownRuleRecords[owner = owner]
```

## Composite role grants

```quarry
use personas alice, bob, carol

table roles:
  principal: principal
  role: enum('coordinator', 'commander')
  unique principal, role
  read <- .principal = viewer or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'commander']
  insert <- exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'commander'] or viewer = 'service:ops'
  delete <- exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'commander']
  online only

table incidents:
  owner: principal
  title: text 1..120
  immutable owner
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'coordinator'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'commander']
  insert <- .owner = viewer
  update <- exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'coordinator'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'commander']
  online only

query canCoordinate():
  return exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'coordinator'] or exists roles[viewer, 'commander']

query canCommand():
  return exists roles[viewer, 'commander']

mutation grant(who: principal, role: enum('coordinator', 'commander')):
  require exists roles[viewer, 'commander'] or viewer = 'service:ops' else NOT_COMMANDER
  return insert roles { principal: who, role }
```


When one principal may hold several independent roles, make the pair unique. Every point probe then supplies both columns. A bare `exists roles[viewer]` is deliberately refused: under `unique principal, role` it names every role row for that principal, not one row. Prefer the named spelling in policy; the full positional spelling, such as `roles[viewer, 'commander']`, has the same meaning. Spell an “any staff role” grant as the `or` of the exact roles that grant it.

Seed the first commander or grant it through `service:ops`. A commander may also have a coordinator row; the pair constraint prevents only a duplicate of the same role. Test both grants separately:

```jsonl
{"as":"alice","op":"canCoordinate","args":{},"data":true,"label":"alice has the coordinator row"}
{"as":"alice","op":"canCommand","args":{},"data":true,"label":"alice also has the commander row"}
{"as":"bob","op":"canCoordinate","args":{},"data":true}
{"as":"bob","op":"canCommand","args":{},"data":false}
```

## Timed disclosure and timed writes
`not before .opensAt` is a server-enforced lower read lifetime. It withholds the row from reads, rule probes and sync until the server reaches the boundary; it does not authorize a write. Keep a write deadline as an explicit mutation `require`. Here submissions close independently, then their secrets become public for the half-open interval `[opensAt, expiresAt)`.

```quarry
use personas alice, bob

table events:
  host: principal
  submitUntil: time
  revealAt: time
  archiveAt: time
  immutable host, submitUntil, revealAt, archiveAt
  public 'event times are public'
  insert <- .host = viewer
  sync public last 500 by .id

table submissions:
  event: events
  author: principal
  opensAt: time
  expiresAt: time
  secret: text 1..2000
  by byOpening: opensAt
  by byExpiry: expiresAt
  immutable event, author, opensAt, expiresAt
  not before .opensAt
  expire at .expiresAt
  public 'submissions are public only during their read lifetime'
  insert <- .author = viewer and .opensAt = .event.revealAt and .expiresAt = .event.archiveAt
  sync public last 500 by .id

mutation submit(event: events, secret: text 1..2000):
  e = events[event] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require now < e.submitUntil else SUBMISSIONS_CLOSED
  return insert submissions { event, author: viewer, opensAt: e.revealAt, expiresAt: e.archiveAt, secret }

//! Expand inherited rules before index planning and aggregate derivation.
// @ref LLP 3000.014#2-the-form — whole-rule substitution preserves every conjunct and service grant.
use super::catalog::TableInfo;
use crate::{ast::*, diag::Diagnostic};
use snapback4_core::schema::through::{CHILDREN, DEADLINE, PARENT_DEADLINE, Through};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
mod paths;
mod hops;

pub(super) fn sync_repair(catalog: &super::catalog::Catalog, info: &TableInfo) -> Option<String> {
    // Keep the authored horizon, before planning adds captured audience columns.
    let decl = catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|doc| &doc.tables)
        .find(|decl| decl.name == info.decl.name).unwrap_or(&info.decl);
    info.through.as_ref().map(|through| paths::repair(decl, &through.reference))
}

fn reference(info: &TableInfo) -> Option<String> {
    info.decl.rules.iter().find_map(|rule| match &rule.value {
        Expr::Call { name, args, .. } if matches!(name.as_str(), "$readThrough" | "$readThroughLive") => match args.as_slice() {
            [Expr::Name { name, .. }] => Some(name.clone()),
            _ => None,
        },
        _ => None,
    }).or_else(|| paths::reference(info))
}

pub(super) fn expand(tables: &mut BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    hops::lower(tables);
    let names: Vec<_> = tables.keys().cloned().collect();
    let mut done = BTreeSet::new();
    for name in names {
        if let Err(d) = one(&name, tables, &mut done, &mut BTreeSet::new()) {
            diagnostics.push(d);
        }
    }
}

fn one(
    name: &str,
    tables: &mut BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>,
    done: &mut BTreeSet<String>,
    visiting: &mut BTreeSet<String>,
) -> Result<(), Diagnostic> {
    if done.contains(name) {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let Some(reference) = reference(&tables[name]) else {
        done.insert(name.into());
        return Ok(());

    }
